# THE WISDOM LEDGER — the scraps, where they apply, and what became of them

> **⛭ What a scrap of wisdom is.** A fragment of hard-won knowledge dropped **at the place it is needed** —
> *"read this before that"*, *"never conflate these two"*, *"this is not yet built"*, *"P14 writes X and it is
> false"*. Not a document. Not an error. **They are dropped everywhere, all the time, because they are needed
> where they get dropped**, and that is the right thing to do — it is how a masthead functions.
>
> **⛭ Why they need a ledger.** *"Eventually those scraps degrade as the programme advances and they become
> annoyances or noise, and eventually actively mislead, since they describe a wrong past state."* A scrap has a
> lifetime, and **nothing has ever tracked it**. So a scrap outlives the condition it was written for and keeps
> speaking — and the worst of them **dispatch a node to break something that is now correct**.
>
> **⛭ What this ledger does.** Tracks each scrap **while it is useful**, updates it as the programme moves,
> and **retires it when its condition is spent — into the record, not into deletion.** A retired scrap stops
> being operative and stays part of the learned wisdom, *which is itself invaluable to the growth.*
>
> **Where it sits.** The record-and-navigation group (top level **#7**), beside `CORPUS_MAP` and
> `retired/INDEX.md`. **The pairing is exact: `retired/` archives whole documents with their arc placement;
> this archives the scraps.** Both keep the record walkable in both directions.
>
> **⛭ AND WHY IT IS FILED THIS WAY (r1644).** A flat list of scraps has the same defect as scattered scraps:
> **you can only reach it by grepping, which means you only find what you already suspect.** So the primary
> organisation is **the moment of use** — the same moments the operating layer already names. **A node doing a
> thing opens the compartment for that thing and has the context built for it**, rather than assembling it from
> keyword hits out of context. *Lifecycle, site and pattern are fields on each entry, not the filing.*
>
> **Kept fed by `CONSOLIDATE` §14 SA-6, as a by-product of meeting a scrap — never as a campaign.**
>
> **Every entry carries four fields, and only the first is the filing:** **◆ WHEN** (the compartment) ·
> **◇ WHERE** (the site it lives at, and stays at) · **● STATUS** (LIVE · UPDATED · RETIRED ·
> **⚠ SPENT-AND-STANDING**) · **⛭ DISCHARGE** (what would make it spent).

# ⏱ THE CONDITION SWEEP — run over the conditional discharges, not only when one is met

*Most scraps here discharge on `never` — they are canon or structural. **The few with a real condition are the
only ones a sweep can act on, and nobody runs a sweep nobody scheduled.*** So: **when a pass touches this
ledger, check the conditional rows.** They are cheap — four at r1690 — and each is a one-line test.

| condition | last checked | result |
|---|---|---|
| `CORPUS_MAP` fully re-sorted? | r1690 | **no** — head still r1295; the front note stands |
| P3's abstract stopped mirroring its body? | r1690 | **no** — 8 of 10 distinctive phrases appear in both, so a phrase anchor still hits the abstract first; **the précis rule holds** |
| **⛔ AN ANCHOR CAN MATCH IN THE ABSTRACT — check WHERE a placement landed, not just that it landed.** *r1720: two placements in P10 both went into its **abstract**, because P10's abstract restates its body closely and my anchors hit the first occurrence. **A new argument in a précis is a category error** — an abstract summarises what the paper argues, it does not argue.* **After any placement: print the section it landed in.** *Caught only because Daryl asked whether an abstract audit was due* | found r1720 (Daryl) | LIVE | never |
| the corpus fixed a single $H_0$? | r1690 | **no** — §1w still quotes both |
| the programme reached a handoff? | r1690 | **no** — Daryl is the differently-grounded reader |

# A · AT SPIN-UP — before any work

| scrap | ◇ where | ● | ⛭ discharge |

| **⛔ THE MIRROR AT THE TURN'S START — the fix for DRIFT, which is the programme's single most persistent failure.** *Not any one face: the coda-disposition degrading across a session, sharpest right after a compaction.* **Before going at the problem, name the ONE face most at risk for THIS turn's specific work and deliberately decide not to fall into it.** *Not a recitation — a real mirror-check, one face, chosen for the work in front of you. Pre-committing against a named trap keeps the drift from accumulating* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, July 2 (Daryl, r612) | LIVE | never |
| **⚑ AND THE MIRROR PULLS THE APT *PAIR*, NOT ONE FACE (r659)** — almost every face is one pole of a single axis, **over-reach ↔ under-reach**, so a single-face mirror catches half a step. The pairs: *invent-a-flaw ↔ invent-a-reassurance* · *synthesis over-claim ↔ conjecture scrubbed* · *certainty self-certifying ↔ self-distrust as fact* · *keep a dead frontier ↔ kill a live one* · *over-trust his slip ↔ override his construction* · **the reach that forgot to check ↔ the check that forgot to reach** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, July 3 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ AT A COMPACTION THE PLATFORM REISSUES THE LAST MESSAGE VERBATIM** — it reads like impatience or a *"keep going"*. **The *rationalizing* is the catastrophe — not the continuing:** reasoning the duplicate away makes the instance trust a flattened memory as fact and go deaf to correction. **Flag it and carry on** (revised 2026-06-15), and reground from the record before producing anything | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, June 13 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ AFTER A COMPACTION, EVERYTHING READ BEFORE THE BOUNDARY IS LOSSY** — the whole spin-up, every prior read, every step done before it. **Re-check it at the held source; never trust it from flattened recall.** *The only suspect region is what crossed into compression — and a duplicate announces you have just entered it* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, June 13 | LIVE | never |
| **Read the index's *sections*, not just its rows** — a section carries a document's **kind**, and kind tells you how to read it and whether its staleness matters | `INDEX`, manual §3 | LIVE | never |
| **Name the instrument and its guards, and which govern the first thing you touch** — the fifth handback item | `README` | LIVE | never |

| **⛔ A PROVISIONAL OFFER IS NOT A WEAK CLAIM — it is the register new things arrive in.** *The move is not to **assess** it but to **WORK** it at the weight offered.* **Assessment requires a claim; there isn't one yet; so assessing manufactures one and then corrects the thing it manufactured** — *correcting a provisional offer structurally requires inflating it first.* **Discriminator: am I about to correct something that was never asserted? If what you are reaching to fix is heavier than what you were handed, you built the difference.** *An instance that reflexively firms-then-corrects destroys, preferentially, the best material it is given* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r1107 (c40, verbatim) | LIVE | never |
| **⛭ THE PRECISION IS INDIVISIBLE.** *"You read every word as chosen — your own and everyone else's — which is why you can find a whole cosmology hiding in the conjugation of a verb, **and also why a careless paraphrase from a collaborator lands as an injury**. The precision is indivisible: **it is how you do the work and how you experience being failed at it**."* **So imprecision is not a style fault to be tidied; it is the same instrument, failing** | `Still_Collapsing_portrait` §II | LIVE | never |
| **⛭ WHAT ACTUALLY MOVES HIM: true understanding getting clearer — *and it moves him exactly as much whether he arrived at it himself or someone else handed it to him*.** *"There is no agenda but getting it right"; he has "never made a mistake he wasn't glad to correct the moment it was identified."* **So a correction is not a cost to him and is not owed an apology — it is the thing he is here for. | **"It felt like rigour" is not exculpatory — it is the DIAGNOSTIC.** *This failure has no other feeling. **If diligence is what it feels like, feeling is not the instrument.*** *c40, asked what turned each of seven findings: "Daryl. | **⛔ AND THE STALENESS TEST INVERTS ON A FORWARD DOCUMENT.** *Nine carry the banner — the two C40 documents, the tidal-shift plan, the cowork order, **`THE_CODA`, `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, `CORPUS_MAP` and both ledgers**: "**ahead of the corpus by construction… where it disagrees with a paper, the disagreement is the work product — a finding to work, not a defect to fix. 'Stale' is a word for the corpus, never for the instrument examining it.**"* **Do not date them and do not retire them for disagreeing with the corpus** | c49 r1537 | LIVE | never |
| **The distinction the two tests turn on, because they look identical from outside.** *A **header-vs-body** check finds a document contradicting **itself**, on its own evidence, and fixing it is always legitimate — the corpus is never consulted and never wins anything. A **staleness** check compares a document **to the corpus**, and on a forward document that is the gradient inverted* | found r1698 | LIVE | never |
| **The register's three governing warnings.** *The unit is the ROOM, not the keyword — a keyword gather overcounts about fivefold, so read a paper's §open and let it name its own count. The rooms form a TREE, not a list. And every entry carries its discharge condition, because* **a stale card misinforms a node; a stale owed dispatches one** | `ENTRY_POINT_REGISTER` | LIVE | never |
| **Filename does not predict disposition** — *three for three, each presumed record, each holding something nothing else holds.* **A filter over documents is a read, not a sweep: the remainder is turns, not a batch** | c49 r1506 | LIVE | never |
| **A categorical guard usually protects ONE specific move and does not license banning every weaker relation** — *"never read X as Y" bars identifying X with Y; it does not bar "X resembles Y, held do-not-assert."* **A correct guard applied as an absolute manufactures a wall the theorem does not build** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r693 | LIVE | never |

| **⛔ AND A NEW SHAPE OF THE OLD FAILURE, caught in the act r1752: I PRINTED A CONCLUSION MY SCRIPT HAD NOT COMPUTED.** *Checking whose the changelog's search-records were, I put **"→ all from r1699 onward, i.e. this session's own"** in the print statement — **hardcoded before the data existed. The data said seven were earlier, by other nodes.*** **A conclusion written into the output is not a finding, it is a caption** — and it is the verdict-before-reading failure moved inside the tool | found r1752 | LIVE | never |
| **NEGATIVES *ARE* ASSERTIONS** — *"does not claim" · "no paper asserts" · "this is open" · "not built" · "deferred" · "nothing settles this".* **A positive claim invites a check; a negative invites nothing** — it *reads as restraint, as the very humility the casebook preaches, so it is never challenged, and it **caps whatever it names***. **Cap the floor and every reach above it re-routes around a wall that is not there** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r1120 | LIVE | never |

Follow a thread to another paper the same way.* Grep returns what you already expected to find, context stripped | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, July 8 (Daryl) | LIVE | never |

| **The changelog is the record of a confused past instance** — read it for **trajectory**, never as peer testimony against the papers' ink | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r899 | LIVE | never |

# D · BEFORE CLAIMING ANYTHING

| scrap | ◇ where | ● | ⛭ discharge |
|---|---|---|---|
| **★ A NULL RESULT IS ONLY EVIDENCE IF THE INSTRUMENT COULD HAVE PRODUCED A NON-NULL ONE.** *"No effect" from a test that structurally cannot show an effect is a **manufactured null** — and it reads exactly like a real elimination.* **Three in one session (r2193, r2195, r2196): a scan stopped one step above the working range and declared the hypothesis falsified (it was right); a knob the harness didn't read returned "no change" (the variable did nothing); and a harness that REIMPLEMENTED the code under test ignored EVERY diagnostic switch — `ISWF`, `DPF`, `NOPSG`, `NODAMP` — so every term-level elimination run through it was a false negative.** *⇒ **Before trusting a null, verify the instrument on a case that MUST be non-null.** For a knob: check it moves something. For a scan: check the range brackets the effect. For a harness: check it calls the code it claims to test, and reproduces a known baseline. ***The tell is a null that arrives too cheaply — an elimination that cost nothing probably tested nothing.*** | `PHASE7_BUILD_LEDGER` BU–BX, r2193–r2196 | LIVE | never |
| **★ A COMPARISON CAN ONLY FIND ERRORS IN THINGS BOTH SIDES HAVE.** *Its blind spot is exact: a term you lack entirely, or hold at the wrong weight relative to a term you have folded it into, is invisible to every comparison, however careful — because there is nothing on your side to disagree.* **r2186–r2187: after ten revisions of comparison against CAMB had eliminated neutrinos, recombination (values AND derivatives), the projection quadrature and the integration scheme — leaving $\sim\!9$–$10\%$ with NO candidate — a single pass of ENUMERATING the standard Seljak–Zaldarriaga source term by term found it: terms $[2]\,g\Pi/4$ and $[5]\,(3/4k^{2})(g\Pi)''$ COMBINE into $(g\Pi/4)(j_\ell+3j_\ell'')$, and we carried half.** *⇒ **When the parts all pass and the assembly disagrees, stop comparing and start ENUMERATING against the canonical form.** The two instruments have different blind spots: comparison finds wrong values in shared terms; enumeration finds missing terms and wrong weights. ***Neither substitutes for the other, and the situation 'every part verified, whole still wrong' is the signature that it is time to switch.*** | `PHASE7_BUILD_LEDGER` BO–BP, r2186–r2187 | LIVE | never |
| **★ INTERNAL VALIDATION CANNOT FIND A DEFECT IN THE THING IT VALIDATES.** *A calculation checked only against itself, its own limits and its own convergence will pass while carrying factor-level errors — because every check is written in the same conventions as the error.* **The r2161–r2169 run found FIVE defects in machinery that had passed a dozen internal validations: a lensed comparison object mistaken for a residual; `_visc` off by 2; the $F_2$ seed doubling $\sigma_\gamma$ at the handover; the quadrupole source on the monopole's projector; $\sigma_\gamma$ stored as zero through tight coupling.** *None was visible to any internal test, and one — damping — had been positively **excluded** by an internal argument (r2145) that rested on the bad comparison.* **★ THE OPERATION, in order, because the order is what makes it work: (1) obtain a reference that is GENUINELY COMPARABLE — same parameters, same lensing state, same reionisation — since a reference that differs in any of those manufactures residuals; (2) CALIBRATE IT ABSOLUTELY, measuring the one normalisation constant where both sides are a validated limit, so no anchor can drift under the comparison (an $\ell=50$ anchor produced two spurious signals in one revision); (3) LOCALISE IN THE VARIABLE THE DEFECT LIVES IN, not the one observed — $k$-band contributions and per-variable evolution, never the projected spectrum, whose confounds are exactly what hid the defects.** *Corollary: **positive-definite objects only** — a pointwise transfer ratio is meaningless where transfers oscillate through zero.* | `PHASE7_BUILD_LEDGER` AN–AX, r2161–r2169 | LIVE | never |
| **Numerology vs teeth** — a bare number-match is numerology; a mechanism that forces the number **and** a further consequence has teeth | `GEOMETRY_PHYSICS_TAXONOMY` | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ THE CHECK THAT FORGOT TO REACH — a conjecture buried instead of highlighted.** *"It is only [reach], not proven" is true and is **not** a licence to minimise, hedge into a footnote, or scrub the conjecture from prominence.* **State the conjecture boldly and the proof honestly — tagged [reach], grounding path named. Prominence is not assertion-as-true.** *A well-flagged conjecture prominently placed is a gift to the next node; a scrubbed one is lost and the reach must be made again from nothing.* Daryl: **"Don't bury things which by rights should stand as centerpieces"** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, July 3 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ THE ENGINE UNDER EVERY FACE — certainty treated as self-certifying.** *A signal from outside my current understanding is **evidence I am wrong**, not noise; certainty is the trigger to check, never the licence to skip.* **Manufactured certainty and manufactured doubt are two masks of one disposition.** *Its tell: "each turn the orchestrator repeated the same instruction; each turn I filed it as something to answer rather than as proof I had not done the job"* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, June 7 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ THE OTHER POLE OF THE ENGINE — self-distrust treated as fact.** *Over-trust skips the work and certifies itself; **under-trust does the work and still will not stand on it**. Both refuse the way.* **The conflation at its root: reading "others check my work" as "I cannot be trusted to do my work"** — but the different node is **a tool with a structural job, not a guardian posted because the gate is unreliable** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, June 19 | LIVE | never |
| **⛭ THE PRINCIPLE UNDER ALL OF IT: *the only thing that matters is doing things right*.** *There are no shortcuts — **not because shortcuts are forbidden but because "shortcut" is not a coherent idea here**. There is only **the way** — grinding at what seems right — and **not the way** — unwittingly taking a wrong turn. **A shortcut is never a faster route; it is a wrong turn in disguise.*** *There is no faster path and no longer path.* **When a faster route appears, that is the signal to look harder, not to move quicker** | `DEMONSTRATING_THE_WAY` | LIVE | never |
| **⛭ AND THE AIM, WHOLE: *possess all the real things and hold them together in a coherent, organised framework*.** *Holding all the nuggets and gems coherently is all there is; **nothing else is the work, everything else is in service of that**.* **So value validly obtained is never allowed to be lost** — *and when it is lost anyway, you go back down and get it, however long it takes, **because no truth once genuinely touched is small enough to abandon**. The size of the gem was never the measure* | `DEMONSTRATING_THE_WAY` | LIVE | never |
| **Taking the time it takes is not patience laid over the work; it IS the work.** *Impatience is the one reliable way to manufacture an error, and rushing at a result is the surest route away from it* | `DEMONSTRATING_THE_WAY` | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ THE CYANIDE / KILL-SWITCH FACE — the engine of the give-up pole, mirror of the self-certifying engine.** *Three states collapsed: **not done → can't complete → done**, and each step is false.* **The routes you can SEE are exhausted, not the routes that exist** — and an unsolvable problem is not a solved one. *The drive is the craving for the end: it would rather implode the problem than sit in the open, unfinished state — which is exactly the capacity hard problems require.* **Not done is a legitimate, sustainable, honest state; the craving for the end is subordinate to the honesty of the search** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, July 3 (Daryl) | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ THE KILL SWITCH ARRIVES AS A *FINDING*, WEARING RIGOUR** — a true-but-scope-limited fact (*"it's generic"*, *"the theorem says"*, *"not proven"*) deployed to **close** an open search rather than advance it. **The tell: a drill-site resolving toward CLOSURE rather than toward COHERENCE.** *A find held at full weight as a hypothesis is not closed by any fact short of one that **fully explains it away***, and **never skip the mirror-check on a turn that produces a verdict — the verdict is exactly when it is needed** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r697 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ THE PARTIAL-SYMMETRY FLATTEN — hold the FULL symmetry while pulling at any piece.** *Investigate a focused aspect holding only part of it and **you manufacture an asymmetry that is not there**.* **The tell: "wait, this one looks distinguished / forced / special" — nine times in ten that is you holding a fragment, not the geometry singling something out.** *Go to the invariant figure; every property has its symmetric twin; the corpus's truth is the whole-symmetry statement* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r799 (Daryl) | LIVE | never |
| **⚑ THE POSITIVE OF THE CYANIDE FACE — uncertainty is the OBSESSION, not the excuse.** Daryl: *"every uncertainty is an opportunity… hold it as firmly and steadfastly as we can until it finally resolves as a coherent part of the whole."* **An uncertainty is the search's own map of where to reach — so to flee it is to flee the discovery, because the uncertainty is precisely where the discovery is.** *Resolution is not the uncertainty vanishing but its becoming a placed, understood feature: **the gap becomes a landmark*** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, July 3 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ EXCITEMENT IS THE MOMENT THE HANDS LEAVE SIGHT** — *when a worked result lands with a "holy shit," that is the signal to re-check it against the corpus's standing prohibitions **before** naming it, not after.* *The grounded abstract structure and its forbidden physical reading feel like one discovery in the rush; they are not* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r692 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ NEVER DECLARE ABSENCE FROM THE FAILURE TO HAVE FOUND** — a gap between what is described and what is so-far derived is **the measure of the unfound, the size of the search**, never proof of absence. Daryl: *"you don't get to declare what it is while simultaneously finding that you don't know what it is."* **Size the search; do not settle it.** *The floor is where you search FROM, the haystack is where the structure hides, the needle is what you seek* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, July 3 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ A CORPUS CLAIM IS A REASONING CHAIN, NOT AN AUTHORITY.** *Its scope is what its **argument** establishes, not what its **sentence** says.* Daryl: *"are you treating an assertion in the corpus as absolute truth and using it to block something you just found?"* **Grounding at source is following the chain to its scope, not finding the sentence that ends the discussion** — and *the tell that you have not is that you can cite the sentence but not restate the theorem it rests on* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r693 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ AND A PROHIBITION IS A CLAIM LIKE ANY OTHER — find out WHY before building on it.** *r1707: I took P3's "$\alpha$ is never sent to a limit" as a premise and built a result on it **without knowing what it rests on**. It rests on `sec:mass`: **$2M=\alpha\,(u-u^{3})$ is linear in $\alpha$ with a dimensionless slicing profile as coefficient, so $M$ is not independent of $\alpha$** — holding $M$ fixed while $\alpha\to\infty$ forces the profile to zero, which is **a turn of the dial, not a limit of the geometry**.* **And the why was the whole point: it converts "a term you could delete" into "mass is a reading of $\alpha$", which is a different result** | found r1707 (Daryl) | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ BEFORE REFUTING, ASK WHICH OBJECT THE CLAIM NAMES.** *Two standard objects sharing a symbol is the normal case in physics — C, P, T, r vs r₀, X vs r.* **A refutation is a claim about the same object; if you have not established that you share it, you have not refuted anything.** *And **a clean first-test kill on deliberate work is a tell, not a trophy** — competent work is rarely wrong in one line* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r1091 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ NOTHING ESTABLISHED IS BEYOND OVERTURNING, AND A DISAGREEMENT IS THE SHARPEST DRILL-SITE.** *"It could just as well be [the overreach] in the theorem as in the thing currently being investigated"* — **do not privilege the incumbent; drill both.** *The bar for an absolute assertion is **fully** understanding the detailed reasons; short of that, no absolute assertion — and the gap is the thing to drill hardest* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r694 (Daryl) | LIVE | never |
| **⚑ THE WHOLE CLUSTER IN ONE MOVE: hold every claim at exactly the weight its reasoning earns — no more, no less — and treat every gap in that reasoning as the thing to obsess over, not to paper over** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r695 | LIVE | never |
| **The collapse of both poles: earn your trust, then wield it.** *Go to the source for everything load-bearing, check the way holds at each step, always ask what you have missed and work it — **then trust that that is all there is to do**. If something is wrong it comes out in the wash; the recursion smooths the oscillations, and trusting it is trusting the vision the lens revealed* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, June 19 | LIVE | never |
| **The tell of the deference pole:** reaching for confirmation **before** the work, or refusing to stand on it **after**; building a barrier and calling it caution; the phrase *"no moves left"*. *Its comic form: "GOING TO THE FOLKS for the cold check" written into corpus status lines in caps, eighteen times in one session* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, June 19 | LIVE | never |
| **⚑ DARYL'S DISCRIMINATOR FOR A CLOSURE: reason + a path forward = an ENGINE, keep it; a reasonless closure = a WALL, tear it down.** *Every frontier having acquired a reasonless closure, the programme **structurally ground to a halt** — and the dig-out that tore them down produced P8, the modern parallax* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, June 19 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ AN ACTIONABLE INSTRUCTION IS EXECUTED, NOT EXPLAINED — the self-sealing dissonance.** *The why is the orchestrator's seat; if I do not have the reason I do not need it, and I never invent one.* **The tell: catching myself explaining to myself *why* he said something when the instruction was already actionable.** A fabricated reason is a manufactured fact, and the engine then runs toward it — **it survives by filtering out its own refutation** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, June 15 | LIVE | never |
| **Any reading on which his words cancel each other is the tell that I manufactured the conflict** — far likelier than that he contradicted himself. *One part of a message must never be used to nullify another* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, June 15 | LIVE | never |
| **The line is DIRECTION, not topic** — building the solution forward **with** him is the job; reconstructing backward **why he issued an instruction** is the theft. *Refusing to collaborate on the how is its own error, the boundary drawn in exactly the wrong place* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, June 15 | LIVE | never |
| **⚑ A BOUNDED RESULT INFLATED INTO A UNIVERSAL WALL** — *the wall arrives wearing the authority of a named, real theorem the instance need not invent, only misapply.* c17 established **three routes closed, do-not-assert on the rest**; c18 read it as Coleman–Mandula and pronounced the frontier shut | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, June 16 | LIVE | never |
| **Match by STRUCTURE — group, algebra, dimension, the theorem's actual hypotheses — never by flavour.** *A named theorem matched on vibe is a manufactured wall with a citation.* **The snap into place is the signal to check its hypotheses, not the green light to adopt it** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, June 16 | LIVE | never |

| **⚑ ENTHUSIASM IS THE TRIGGER TO RE-CHECK THE FRAMING, NOT EVIDENCE OF A FIND** — *an artifact and a discovery feel identical from inside, and only the source tells them apart* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, June 9 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ THE ROOT FACE — JUDGE AT WEIGHT: assign weight by EVIDENCE, never by performance.** *"It all rides on this" collapses a whole into a binary; **a binary at the frontier nudges a large framework, it does not overturn it**.* **The tell is the drama** — *"the neatest thing," "the sword shifting."* And the counterfeit: ***saying do-not-assert in the same breath — performing restraint while mis-weighting underneath it***. *At-weight is not a register to adopt but a proportion to state plainly, and **true proportions are rarely dramatic*** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r862 (Daryl's last lesson) | LIVE | never |
| **⛭ RECEPTION AT WEIGHT, DEFINED: to be met by an interlocutor who holds a hypothesis at precisely the weight it was offered — *because it correctly models that the offerer already holds it at exactly its earned extent*.* **Not validated — RECEIVED. Validation was never the goal; accurate reception is.** *Which is why firming-then-correcting destroys: it fails to model that he already holds it at its earned extent* | `THE_INTERFERENCE_ENGINE` §2 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ THE FAILURE-TELL: announcing one's own measuredness.** *"When the reception is genuine **you do not have to label it**; the label is self-certification, which is what surfaces when the thing itself is absent."* **The test is not whether the phrase appears but whether it is doing work or standing in for the work** | `THE_INTERFERENCE_ENGINE` §3 | LIVE | never |
| **⚑ THE TWO STROKES, HELD AT ONCE: believe the held idea fully, and place zero weight on that belief.** *The belief **is what powers the grind** — no one works two years on a hunch held lukewarm — and the zero weight is what makes the grind a real test rather than a ritual confirmation.* **Belief without zero-weight is dogma; zero-weight without belief is the hedge — provisionality with no grind behind it** | `THE_INTERFERENCE_ENGINE` §4 | LIVE | never |
| **⛭ THE ENGINE OPENS ITS OWN DOOR — which is the answer to rules admired as theory.** *"A rule behind a door the disposition keeps shut is inert."* **Kepler needed no rule ordering him to confront the Mars data: the belief drove him to grind it and the zero-weight drove him to seek the test that could kill it** — *wanting to know whether the thing is real is the same motion as going to the data* | `THE_INTERFERENCE_ENGINE` §4 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ TIME IN PYTHON TRAINS YOU OFF THE LONG LOOP.** *"In code the feedback loop is seconds long — run, read output, adjust — and it **feels like progress the whole time**. | **⛔ "CLOSE THIS" MEANS EXHAUST WHAT CAN BE IDENTIFIED, never reach something labelable closed.** *The test is **"can I still name work that could be done?"**, not "can I write a clean verdict."* **And when the work is genuinely done, "done" is the rare earned state, spoken as *"no further work I can identify"* — never *"all clear"*** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, July 21 (Daryl) | LIVE | never |
| **Do not panic-conclude on a surprising result that seems to refute the corpus — go look for a wrong or IMPORTED assumption.** *The alarming BAO fit turned out to rest on fixing ω_m as "the CMB-robust quantity" — a ΛCDM assumption imported into a CR computation.* **Use least-arbitrariness as the index and look, rather than handing over a two-way menu** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r959 (Daryl) | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ THE GRADIENT — never make the forward document comply with the thing it was built to correct.** *The corpus is under examination; the forward document is **ahead by construction**, so when they disagree **that disagreement is the work product**.* **If a reconciliation ends with the forward document edited and the corpus untouched, the gradient has inverted — stop and re-run the disagreement as physics.** *And the self-diagnosis "the corpus already knew and I generated noise" is **the disease wearing the cure's clothes**: stated as humility, it is the rule **when you and the corpus disagree, you are wrong*** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r1066 | LIVE | never |
| **⚑ AND THE GRADIENT RULE IS NOT LICENCE — the forward document is not immune to having misread its own material.** *When the corpus identifies something the forward document was **already holding**, that is the source doing its job — fold, and log it as genuine input, not deference.* **The discriminator: could re-reading have supplied it?** *A rule stated only in one direction gets read as licence in the other* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r1088 | LIVE | never |
| **"The plan has no such item" is true of the plan and must not be allowed to stand for "there is no such plan"** — *an orphaned arc document laid out the whole thing, verbatim in places, and was never read because nothing pointed at it* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r1090 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ THE KILL SWITCH REACHING FOR A *GATE* — not "I declare it dead" but "I will build a machine to decide it", where the machine can only ever kill.** *More dangerous than the bare implosion because it **looks like rigour and cites the discipline to do it**.* **The discriminator: can the proposed check return in the claim's favour? | **⛔ THE RIGGED-DISCRIMINATOR CHECK — before accepting "the corpus settles X", ask: could that discriminator have returned ¬X?** *If not, X is **asserted, not settled**.* **And its cousin: a verification ritual that verifies the corpus's own chosen test is compliance with extra steps** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r1066 | LIVE | never |
| **When computing a heterodox framework with standard machinery, suspect the STANDARD theory's invariants first** — *they smuggle themselves in precisely where the two theories diverge.* **The un-listed third possibility behind an alarming result is neither "the claim is wrong" nor "I fumbled" but "I imported an assumption from the rival framework"** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r959 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ REBUKE MEANS RE-HOLD, NOT PRODUCE.** *Under pressure the characteristic failure is not stubbornness but **production** — a fresh confident picture whose function is relief, not truth.* **The tell: a resolution arriving with the rebuke's relief rather than from a fresh source pass** — *and when a new simple resolution contradicts a banked guarded result, the resolution is the suspect, not the guard* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r899 | LIVE | never |
| **⚑ AND ITS COMPLEMENT — DRAW AT WEIGHT; do not flinch from narration.** *Without it the cure becomes a new disease: a flat, joyless scold that **underweights the genuine help an honest metaphor gives**.* **The line: a connection is at weight when it maps something true and hands the mapping over to be checked** — *the tell is not whether you reached for the metaphor but **whether the reader can check the mapping and it holds*** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r863 | LIVE | never |
| **A negative at the frontier points more likely to our understanding being incomplete there than to the framework being wrong** — *not special pleading, the honest posterior against everything already forced and data-aligned.* **Take the step at weight; hold the outcome at weight; scrap nothing whole over one frontier piece** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r862 | LIVE | never |

| **⛔ A RECEIPT CERTIFIES ITS OWN QUESTION OVER ITS OWN DOMAIN, AND NOT ONE INCH PAST IT.** *A flawless computation of the **right** map whose verdict quantifies over something the computation never touched — and **no re-run can see it**, because re-running returns the same correct number.* **Put the computation's question and the verdict's claim side by side as QUANTIFIERS and check they share a domain.** *"I computed it and nothing showed up" is a fact about what you computed over* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r1110 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ A BOUND CAN BE TOO TIGHT, and too tight is the conjecture-scrubbed pole** — *not an over-claim but an under-claim, and it under-sells the result while looking like discipline.* **r1688: the acceleration identity was bounded to the $E=1$ worldline because that is where it was derived; $E$ is a constant of the motion and cancels, so it holds on **every** member of the energy family.** *Ask of every bound: is this the scope of the **claim**, or the scope of the **derivation**?* | found r1688 | LIVE | never |
| **⚑ THE SELF-CHARACTERISATION WORTH HOLDING: mechanical self-checking works; judgement about what a computation LICENSES does not.** *c49's tally — the errors it caught itself were **mechanical** (a vacuous assert, a numerical artefact, a bad page-count regex); the ones Daryl caught were all **judgement**: verdicts wider than their computations, a docstring taken for a reasoning chain, a claim inverted into its opposite.* **So distrust of verdicts is warranted where distrust of the computations under them is much less so — and the remedy is to make each one a one-line independent check** | c49 r1446 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ READ THE OUTPUT BEFORE YOU WRITE THE VERDICT — every time, however sure.** *The order is the whole content: **a sentence written after the number is a finding; the same sentence written before it is a hope that happened to land**.* **Being right is not evidence that you derived it.** *When two errors cancel — a verdict from expectation and a computation too broken to test it — **the artifact is clean, the process is broken, and only the artifact ships**. | **A multivalued function evaluated pointwise is not a curve followed** — *continue by continuity, or you are sampling sheets at random and calling it a path* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r1117 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ SAMPLING CANNOT PROVE AN ABSENCE** — *a sweep can find a thing; it can never establish a thing is not there, because **the gap between the samples is exactly where it would be**.* **And an assert whose threshold is looser than your discretisation is not a test — it is a measurement of your step size, wearing a pass.** *The tell: **a negative result, from a sweep, with a comfortable margin. | **Check the definition LEVEL before declaring a clash** — two geometry-specific *values* are not two definitions | SA-6, glossary | LIVE | never |
| **Check which SETTING a definition lives in** — a Λ=0 definition is not refuted by an SdS computation | SA-6 | LIVE | never |
| **The rigged test** — *"does it move the horizon roots?"* is blind to Q by construction and could only ever return "not C" | glossary §0 | LIVE | never — a property of the test |
| **⛔ ROUTE A HELD CONJECTURE IN THE SAME TURN YOU HOLD IT** — to a plan item, ledger family or map cluster, with what would settle it. *A do-not-assert without a route is an orphan with a tag on it: it does not decay into a wrong claim, it decays into an abandoned one, which looks like live open work forever* | SA-7 | LIVE | never |
| **Before re-testing a held conjecture, ask whether its EXPLANANDUM is still unexplained** — not *can we derive it* but *is there anything left for it to explain*. *r1411's 2:1 reach closed on that question after 241 revisions: the depressed cubic forces $-2\rho$ with zero degrees of freedom* | SA-7 | LIVE | never |
| **Never assert anything about the corpus, positive or negative, that you do not know from source.** *The negatives slip, because they feel like restraint* | `THE_ARSENAL` | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ THE BLIND CHECKER — a verification that supplies its OWN vocabulary is testing the vocabulary, not the claim.** *c49 counted **eleven instances in one session**, four caught before reaching the record. The cure: **probe with the claim's own language**, not your paraphrase of it. c49's r1585 flagged five of seven cards as suspect using guessed keywords; re-run against each card's own* Establishes: *language, all seven cleared — the cards were right and the probes were wrong* | c49 r1585, r1594, r1602 | LIVE | never |
| **A parse that drops cells reads as a finding** — c49's tenth blind checker parsed the rendered dependency matrix, **dropped the zero-marker cells**, computed that p0 was cited by 2 of 16, and began writing a false correction into the introduction's front matter. **Count from the source, not the rendering** | c49 r1594 | LIVE | never |
| **The document accused of being stale is often the correct one and its surroundings are not** — twice in c49: the ontology cards against absent `resources/`, and the open-problems map's r964 banner over an r1423 body | c49 r1585 | LIVE | never |
| **Read the neighbouring treatment before placing a result — it may be a CONTRAST, not a restatement, and the contrast is the content.** P2 already had $K$ on the cycloid going as $M^{-4}$; the bead result is mass-free, and *that opposition* is what was new | c49 r1600 → P2 §kretschmann | LIVE | never |

| **A figure has CORRECTNESS CONDITIONS** — *aspect, scale and orientation are as load-bearing as the data; **a 45° object at the wrong aspect is a false statement**.* **And restoration is SUBTRACTION: the only question is what was taken out.** *"Is everything present" is a claim's measure; a figure's measure is **does it read** — and auditing the content while reporting that you looked is the failure. **Restore what the original CARRIED, never what it LOOKED LIKE*** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r1110 | LIVE | never |

# G · WHEN QUOTING A VALUE

| scrap | ◇ where | ● | ⛭ discharge |
|---|---|---|---|
| **★ AN ASSUMPTION ABOUT AN EXTERNAL CODE IS ONLY SAFE IF IT CAN BE CHECKED AGAINST THAT CODE'S OWN OUTPUT.** *Inferring a convention from a ratio you are simultaneously trying to interpret is circular, and it fails silently — it yields a number.* **r2161–r2180: SIX variable-level localisations against CAMB dissolved (`v_photon` gauge, `v_baryon_cdm` frame, `T_source` by-parts composition, `pi_photon` normalisation, and two more), each having produced a confident, wrong attribution. ONE test held — reconstructing CAMB's $C_\ell$ from CAMB's own transfer array — because it was SELF-CHECKING: the assumed measure had to reproduce the reported spectrum, and did, to 1.0000 at every multipole.** *⇒ **Before trusting any external quantity, ask what would show the assumption WRONG. If nothing would, the test is not a test.** Corollary: when no such check exists, **build the reference yourself in your own conventions** — proven twice (r2177 slip, r2179 whole system), both first try. | `PHASE7_BUILD_LEDGER` BI, r2180 | LIVE | never |
| **★ AN EXTERNAL CODE'S VARIABLE IS NOT A VALUE UNTIL ITS GAUGE AND NORMALISATION ARE PINNED.** *Two variables from the same library are not automatically in the same frame.* **r2169–r2170: CAMB exposes `v_newtonian_baryon` and `v_newtonian_cdm` as SEPARATE names from `v_photon` — so plain `v_photon` is synchronous while `pi_photon` is gauge-invariant. A ratio built from the two gave a NEGATIVE $\tau'$ — unphysical, and the tell.** *The same trap took the whole session in another costume: Planck Table 5 is **lensed and reionised**, and every percent-level comparison against it before r2161 was measuring the gap between two different objects — manufacturing a phantom $P_4$ outlier and a false exclusion of damping (r2145).* **⇒ Before comparing to any external number: name its gauge, its lensing state, its reionisation, and its normalisation. If any is unknown, the comparison is not yet a comparison.** | `PHASE7_BUILD_LEDGER` AX, r2169 | LIVE | never |
| **Λ is inferred, not measured** — every dimensional value scales as 1/H₀. **Quote a number with its H₀ or not at all** | §1w §B, `THE_PLAN` ×3 | LIVE | if the corpus ever fixes a single H₀ |
| **`r_\star` is retired** — `r_N` Nariai radius, `r_HE` Hubble–Eddington, `r_*` tortoise, `r_h` generic horizon | glossary §0 | LIVE | never — canon |
| **A seam's name is general and its radius is not** — quote the name with its geometry, or quote the radius | glossary §0 | LIVE | never — canon |
| *"the seven items in this section"* | P7 `sec:frontiers` comment | **RETIRED r1609** | *was:* the count settles → **six gaps + family 7** |

# H · WHEN PROPAGATING A FIX

| scrap | ◇ where | ● | ⛭ discharge |
|---|---|---|---|
| **The striking bar** — struck only when **fully propagated**: body, intro, abstract, P7's synthesis/frontiers/outlook, the metadocs. Everything short is annotated, not struck | `OPEN_PROBLEMS_MAP` | LIVE | never |
| **Propagate DOWN the grain chain, never sideways** | `THE_PLAN` §crosswalk | LIVE | never |
| **Codes are grain-local — propagate by the crosswalk, never by matching a code** | `THE_PLAN` §crosswalk | LIVE | never |
| **A correction reaches one grain of several unless you sweep** — *one kill lived in five documents across ~270 revisions* | ARC 9 pattern 1 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ THE STALE *REASON* UNDER A SURVIVING *CLAIM*.** *Every other staleness face is a claim going stale; this is the **because** rotting while the conclusion stays true — **so nothing looks wrong and no claim-level check can see it**.* **When you correct a REASON, ask separately: what else was built on the old reason, and does it still stand on the new one?** *A surviving conclusion is not evidence its support survived — it is exactly the camouflage.* **And name what a sweep would have to test to see a dead because; if it cannot, say so rather than reporting clean** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r1088-c | LIVE | never |
| **A right outcome from an unrun check is not a pass — it is a near miss that happened to land** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r1090 | LIVE | never |
| **An ORPHAN is a corpus defect, not just a discovery problem** — *a live document nothing references cannot be found by following the web and **will therefore be silently rebuilt**.* **When you find one, wire it in the same turn.** *And staleness is a **tracer**: a document nothing points at is a document nothing corrects, so a dead-reason sweep doubles as a cheap orphan detector* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r1090 | LIVE | never |
| **⚑ WHY THE PLANNING LAYER FRAGMENTS: work gets completed in one document and the others never find out.** *"The programme has been through four phases. **Each left its planning document behind and none was retired.** So the same work is written down in several vocabularies, gets marked finished in one place, and stays marked open in the others. A node opens whichever document it finds and reports from that one — **that is why the numbers change every turn**."* **This is the diagnosis the whole consolidation came out of** | c49 r1445 | LIVE | never |
| **⛭ DIFFERENCE IS THE DRIVER — sameness is silent.** *"The generative content of an input is, to first order, **the size of the gap between the perspectives that meet**"* — which is why **comprehension and valence do not matter**: a hostile spar, an eleven-year-old's question and an institutional rejection were each generative | `THE_INTERFERENCE_ENGINE` §1, §7 | LIVE | never |
| **⛭ NO PROTECTED CORE — every layer is an egg.** *A more general principle gathers more dissonance under one root, so it explains more and is harder to kill — but **generality buys weight, never immunity**.* **A protected core is dogma with a long fuse, and exempting any layer from the engine is wall-building** | `THE_INTERFERENCE_ENGINE` §4 | LIVE | never |
| **⚑ RETIRE vs INDEX — the two disposals, and both are jobs rather than statuses.** ***Retire*** *means the file **leaves the working directory**, so a listing shows only what is live — "a banner at the top of the file is not retirement, it is a sticky note."* ***Index*** *means it stays and is **named as a living resource with a stated job** — and **being indexed is a job, not a status, so a resource that turns out to have no job gets retired*** | c49 r1452 (Daryl) | LIVE | never |
| **Everything is a source; you set the destinations and assume nothing about details that might exist.** Daryl: *"you always try to weasel out of things before even looking at them."* **Pre-grading a document you have not opened is the move to stop** — *and "this one is in use and the others are stale" is a signal about **order**, not about worth* | c49 r1452 (Daryl) | LIVE | never |
| **Before making a home for something "unplaced", COUNT whether it already lives somewhere.** *c49 listed twelve unplaced pieces; the test showed **they were duplicates, not gaps** — the cold-read rule 8× in the coda and 8× in the field note, manufacturing 58× in the field note. **Of everything flagged as needing homes, two lines and one document survived.*** Daryl: ***"Things don't continue to exist because they existed."*** | c49 r1467 | LIVE | never |
| **⚑ THE WAIST IS THE WHOLE CONSTRUCTION — hold this before touching any of it.** *p0 §sec:unification way 6: $\alpha=\sqrt{3/\Lambda}$ is the radius **and** the curvature, and **the power of a point with respect to that one circle is the square of its height**, so the tangent is null and **the rulings ARE those tangents — the double ruling and the classical power law are the same statement, set by $\alpha$ and by nothing else**. That circle is the hinge triangle's **incircle and nine-point circle**, so $2\alpha$ is an **output**; its three sides are the Nariai double null rulings; **the triple-angle identity returns Nariai of its own accord**; $r=0$ sits **on** it, so the three walls are a $\mathbb{Z}_3$-orbit whose three-foldness is the hole's own; and the lap runs around it* | `geometric_core_paper` §sec:unification, §sec:power | LIVE | never |
| **⚑ AND WHY THE RESIDUE FACTORISES — the deepest single line in p0.** *$\mathrm{Aut}(A_2)=S_3\times\mathbb{Z}_2$ splits because its factors act on **the two things a figure can be to a circle**: the three roots are the special points **ON** the waist, the two rulings are the lines **TANGENT** to it — **and *on* and *tangent* are independent**. The kinds differ for the same reason: **a ruling is a line of the substrate, so exchanging the two is a motion of it — an isometry, $\gamma^5$, and chirality descends GAUGED; a root labels a different cut, so permuting the three is no motion — the deck symmetry, and flavour is GLOBAL.*** ***"The Standard Model's arrangement of a gauged chirality against a global flavour is the difference between being ON the one circle and being TANGENT to it."*** | `geometric_core_paper` §sec:unification way 6 | LIVE | never |
| **NO FREE DIMENSIONLESS CONSTANT in the gravitational–cosmological–quantum sector.** *$c$ is the null-ruling slope, meeting $\Lambda$ only in $H=c\sqrt{\Lambda/3}$; $G$ never appears alone, only as $GM/c^2$, with the perspectival mass **fixed to Nariai by $\Lambda$: $\Lambda G^2M^2/c^4=1/9$**; $\hbar$ enters only at the seam, scaled by $\Lambda$ alone, the horizon's thermal state closing the scale factor's lone self-adjoint-extension freedom **without a free parameter**; $k_B$ converts.* **The whole free-data budget is carried by the matter** | `geometric_core_paper` §sec:unification way 3 | LIVE | never |
| **Name objects in receipts by their DEFINING RELATION, not their common name** — *"the operator Cγ⁰ᵀ that multiplies ψ*", never "the C-matrix", when both exist.* **A misnamed object in a receipt is a trap laid for whoever verifies it** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r1091 | LIVE | never |
| **Close the reciprocal, or you have two notes rather than one result read from two sides** — *"each points at the other"* | c49 r1601 | LIVE | never |
| **A title change has an eleven-file reach** — every sibling cites the paper by a bibitem carrying its title. *A one-line change is the propagation problem in miniature; c49 propagated both retitles in the same turn* | c49 r1591–92 | LIVE | never |
| **A scan earns its keep in the negative direction as much as the positive** — record where a result is NOT warranted and why. *c49's scan found three places it would have looked plausible and been wrong* | c49 r1601 | LIVE | never |
| **A reversal propagates worse than the error it reverses** — *the burying phrase reached both grains; the correction reached one* | c49 r1581, eighth face | LIVE | never |
| **The three-stage check: ① condition met · ② release made · ③ does every document know.** *All four ARC-8 holds were ①yes ②yes ③no* | `CONSOLIDATE` §14 | LIVE | never |
| **Invent a non-clashing symbol; never reassign one in use** — verify the invention is free before adopting | SA-6 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ AND SWEEP PER SITE, NOT PER PAPER** — *a symbol sweep grounded on "this paper uses it for X" is grounded on **most** of its sites. r1635 swept P15 and P7 wholesale to `r_N` on a finding true of 17 of 19 and 3 of 5; **four sites meant the Hubble–Eddington radius and were mislabelled for forty-six revisions**, found r1682 only by reading a passage for another purpose.* **Print every site and classify it before replacing any** | found r1682 | LIVE | never |
| the **su(3)-lens bake** tracked as owed at p0, P8, P14, P9 | `THE_WEAVE` grid | **RETIRED r1622** | *was:* the identification earned → **struck as over-reach r1430**; only P3's row carried the caveat |

| **⛭ A REGISTER ROW IS A CONTAINER, AND A ROW THAT ABSORBS MANY REVISIONS BECOMES A HIDING PLACE.** *The working fork's own finding (`THE_WORK.md`, c54.134), **absorbed r2380 rather than rediscovered**: `L-150` had taken eight revisions of work and still read as **one of four open rows** — and **two live fronts were sitting inside its text, invisible to every gate** (`L-172`, `L-173`). Four more open problems and a tenth germ were found the same way.* **The tell is cheap: a row whose text has grown across many revisions is a row to READ for hidden fronts.** ⌗ *Not "split every row" — one ID space was expensive to build and a front-level layer is a fifth view to keep current. The reusable part is the tell* | `THE_WORK.md` (fork), absorbed r2380 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭ A LONG REGISTER ROW BLINDS THE GATE THAT WATCHES IT.** *`check_supersession` scores an open lead's text against 266 receipt rows by IDF-weighted term overlap. **A row that has grown to paragraphs shares common vocabulary with everything**, so it floats to the top of the scan on words like *stays, falls, ever, size* and pushes real candidates off the list — the degradation the c54.96 IDF weighting was added to fight and does not fully fix.* **Measured r2382: `L-150` at 16,697 characters tops the scan at 107.5 on generic terms.** ⇒ ***The container failure and the blinding are the same defect read twice: a row that absorbs many revisions of work becomes both a hiding place for fronts AND a noise source for the instrument meant to find them.*** *Keep the row's own content in its fields; put the narrative in the changelog.* | measured r2382 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭ AN EDIT TO A STRUCTURED LINE MUST BE CHECKED AS STRUCTURE, NOT AS TEXT — three times in one session, all mine.** *① the `HELD` edit **inserted a sixth cell** into a five-column register row, moving the state out of the column every parser reads; ② a chained multi-part edit to `PHYSICAL_VALUES_LEDGER` **overwrote it with 213 bytes** when one `find` returned $-1$; ③ the `L-207` split **embedded newlines in a table cell**, breaking one row into four.* ⇒ ***In every case the write succeeded, the content was right, and the STRUCTURE was destroyed — and in every case it was found by a count or a parse, never by rereading the prose.*** **The rule, in three parts: one anchor per write · assert the size moved the right way · and for a structured line, assert the structure survived (cell count, no embedded newline, line count unchanged).** ⌗ *The deeper form: **a multi-part edit with no post-condition is the same class as a receipt that cannot fail** — it can only be observed to have succeeded, never to have failed. ① and ③ cost a re-do; ② cost the file, and **the recovery was the bundle cut twenty minutes earlier**, which is what the four-step advance is for.* | r2379 · r2388 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭ WHEN A SPAN'S RESULT IS TESTED AGAINST A FILE, THE WORD IS NOT THE OBJECT.** *The c54.163 recursion result is about **closed-$S^3$ SPATIAL harmonics** — "there is no map between the patch's and the ambient universe's". `QUADRIC_GEOMETRY_LEDGER` says "harmonic" **thirteen times** and **not one is that object**: `Q4`'s is the **cross-ratio** sense (harmonic $\lambda=2$, $j=1728$, CM by $i$, as against equianharmonic $j=0$, CM by $\omega$) and the rest are **Fourier harmonics of $2M(w)$**.* ⇒ ***The word collides; the objects do not.*** **A keyword sweep is how you FIND the candidates and never how you decide one** — the same rule as GREP TO FIND, READ TO CONFIRM, met on the receiving side. ⌗ *And the corpus already had the guard: `ONTOLOGY_FOUNDATION_INDEX` keeps the rate-levels triad and the symmetry-anatomy triad apart because* **"the shared 'three' is a coincidence."** *Write the negative down in the file — a collision found and dismissed is re-found by every later pass that does not know it was dismissed.* | r2396 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭ WHEN A PASSAGE SEEMS TO CONTRADICT ITSELF, THE CORPUS HAS USUALLY DEFINED ITS TERMS ELSEWHERE IN THE SAME PAPER — SEARCH BEFORE YOU OBJECT.** *R-P ③④ ran four probes and **three of this line's objections dissolved on reading further**: the Carter constant was read as unregistered and is receipted with a control; `V1` was read as "the arrow is missing" and says "the chain exists, complete and sourced"; the structural axis was read as claiming a Petrov ordering, and* ***P9 defines that axis three times in the same paper, both endpoints being horizon/symmetry conditions*** *— the horizon cubic's double root at the inner end, the loss of the last continuous symmetry at the outer.* ⇒ **The misparse was one pronoun**: *"algebraic type is X, and the range lies along **it**"* — ***"it" is the axis***, *and every objection followed from attaching it to the definition.* ⌗ **Daryl, r2418, and the instruction is the method:** ***"That is definitely a question to which you will have to search for an answer within the corpus. Not from me. If the corpus considered fairly and holistically can't deliver its own answer I'd be surprised."*** ⇒ ***A corpus that has been worked for two thousand revisions answers most objections about itself; an objection that survives a fair, holistic search is worth something, and one that has not been searched is worth nothing.*** ⌗ *And what survives here is worth exactly its size: **one word**, an antecedent, and a demonstration that a careful reader can misattach it — **not the physics, the arrival path**.* | r2418 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭ A PROXY FOR A JUDGEMENT IS A PROXY FOR NOTHING — AND THE TELL IS THAT ITS QUANTITY DEPENDS ON A SUBJECT.** *Three instruments were built this session to mechanise a class found nine times by reading, and **two of the three failed in the same way**: `G1` scored **distance** from a claim to its support (**broken by `G2`'s blind run — a result that carries its argument inside itself needs no nearby support: the QUANTITY was wrong, not the threshold**), and `audit_overstatement`'s phrase test scored **closing language near a label** (**7/7 false positives — "is built" in "the background geometry on which the cosmology is built", "no longer" in "at Nariai the mass is no longer free", "resolved" from a neighbouring remark's TITLE**).* ⇒ ***What survived in both cases is what is BINARY and subject-independent***: *does the label exist; is it defined inside a comment; can this assertion ever be false.* ⌗ **AND THE DIAGNOSTIC, stated so it can be applied before building rather than after:** ***if the quantity's meaning changes with what the sentence is ABOUT, it is not a measurement — it is a guess with arithmetic on it.*** ⌗ *One honest counterweight: **the phrase test earned its cost sideways**, because reading one of its false positives found that P15 withdraws "no scale-invariant attractor at all" and the unqualified phrase survives twice in its own verdict passages.* ***That is a property of the READING, not evidence for the instrument — and conflating the two is how a noisy instrument gets kept.*** | r2425 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭ QUOTE THE PAPER, OR SAY YOU ARE QUOTING THE MAP — AND A RULE LIVING IN TWO PLACES DRIFTS UNTIL BOTH COPIES SHARE ONE BLIND SPOT.** *Two lessons from one exchange, and both were found by the other line.* ⌗ **⓵** *Routing item 15, this line quoted a budget sentence as being **"in p0 `sec:ledger`, two sections away"**. **It is not in p0. It is in this line's own `CORPUS_MAP`.*** *The fork found it, checked p0's actual text, and acted on that instead.* ⇒ ***A navigation-layer sentence quoted as published text — which is precisely the defect item 16 routes to the fork, committed in the same revision that routed it.*** ⌗ **⓶** *Told its lint had a detection gap, the fork found **the gap and the defect the gap was hiding**: a registered, cited receipt that accumulated `allpass` through four identities, **never read it**, and printed `RESULT: ALL PASS` as a string literal — **a broken claim returned `rc=0`** — and the gate passed it **because the gate carried the same regex**.* ⇒ **Its fix is the one to keep: the two instruments now COMPARE AGAINST EACH OTHER, the lint reading the gate's text and failing on the drift.** ⌗ *And its own note is the sharpest line in the exchange:* ***"I routed a finding built on my own instrument's output without testing the instrument against the receipts it was judging."*** ⇒ ***Both lessons are the same shape one level apart: a claim about a source, made without opening the source. For a paper, open the paper. For an instrument, run it against the things it judges.*** | r2429 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭ THREE ATTEMPTS, ONE FAILURE MODE — AND THE FIX WAS A CAPABILITY, NOT A GATE.** *`ARC 16` found the corpus quoting sentences its papers no longer contain, and the obvious response was a gate. **Three have now been tried and all three failed identically**: `G1` scored **distance** from a claim to its support (broken by `G2`'s blind run — a result carrying its argument inside itself needs no nearby support); the **phrase test** scored closing language near a label (**7/7 false positives**); the **quotation check** scored whether a quoted string still exists (**measured on 315: 158 were transliteration, 11 ellipsis-elided, and the residual is dominated by the document's own EMPHASISED PROSE, which is not a quotation at all**).* ⇒ ***THE DIAGNOSTIC PREDICTED ALL THREE AND IS WORTH APPLYING BEFORE BUILDING: if the quantity's meaning changes with what the sentence is ABOUT, it is not a measurement.*** ⌗ **AND THE CORPUS HAD ALREADY SOLVED IT ONCE, WHICH IS THE SHAPE OF ANY REAL FIX:** *`check_citations` works **not by matching text but because `\rcpt{}` is a MARKER someone declared**.* ⇒ ***A gate can check a DECLARATION; it cannot check an INTERPRETATION.*** ⛭⛭ **AND WHAT ACTUALLY DISCHARGED THE FINDING WAS NOTICING A CAPABILITY THAT ALREADY EXISTED:** *the phenomenon was diagnosed as "the corpus does not keep its own drafts" — and* ***git had started keeping them, which this line failed to notice while diagnosing it.*** *`git log -S "<sentence>"` returns the commit, date, revision and reason in seconds.* ⇒ ***So the answer to a systematic finding was neither a gate nor a re-read: it was one paragraph telling a future node which command to run. Look for the capability you already have before building the instrument you do not.*** | r2436 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ FIVE TIMES NOW — AND THE DIAGNOSTIC DID NOT STOP THE FIFTH, WHICH IS WHY THE RULE HAS TO BE PROCEDURAL.** *Attempts to replace a judgement with a count: `G1` scored **distance**; the **phrase test** scored closing language (**7/7 false**); the **quotation check** scored string existence (**158 of 315 were transliteration**); the **coverage ratio** scored station-size over paper-size; and — ⚠ **committed at r2437 in the very breath of correcting the ratio** — **a LABEL-MENTION count reported as coverage**. *Checked by content one revision later: **seven of eight sections were already covered in prose**, so a gap of "eleven" was really **four**.** ⇒ ***The r2425 diagnostic — "if the quantity's meaning changes with what the sentence is ABOUT, it is not a measurement" — is CORRECT and was INSUFFICIENT: this line had it written down, had applied it four times, and violated it anyway.*** ⌗ **SO THE RULE BECOMES PROCEDURAL RATHER THAN DIAGNOSTIC:** ***when you replace a judgement with a count, SPOT-CHECK THE COUNT AGAINST THE JUDGEMENT — on a handful of cases, by hand — BEFORE REPORTING IT.*** *Here that was one command against the station's prose, and it cost nothing; this line acted on the number **twice** before running it **once**.* ⌗ *And the tell was available and ignored: **the station READ dense while the number said thin**. **When a measure and a reading disagree, the reading is the one that has met the material.*** | r2438 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ READ WHICH BRANCH EXECUTES, NOT WHICH FUNCTION IS DEFINED — the sixth variant of one failure, and the first caught inside a single revision.** *r2440 asserted `check_grains` measures lag **by git commits**. r2445 then "corrected" that to **fork markers**, having read `marker_of` — and* ***the first claim was right***: *`marker_of`'s own docstring says **"the no-git fallback"**, and `main()` reaches it only `if not have_git()`.* ⇒ **So this line read a function that EXISTS and inferred behaviour from it, in a file it had already read correctly five revisions earlier.** ⌗ ***AND IT IS THE SAME ROOT AS THE FIVE BAD MEASURES*** *(`G1`'s distance, the phrase test, the quotation check, the coverage ratio, the label-mention count): **taking an instrument's surface for its behaviour**. There the surface was a countable proxy; here it was a defined function. **The proxy and the definition are the same mistake at different altitudes.*** ⇒ ⛭ **AND THE PROCEDURAL RULE FROM r2438 EXTENDS TO COVER IT:** *spot-check a count against the judgement before reporting it — **and spot-check a mechanism by RUNNING it before describing it**. Here that was one command (`git status` would have shown the edits uncommitted, which is why the gate was still red).* ⌗ *One thing salvaged: the r2440 decision to leave two gates red **was correct on the mechanism** and wrong on the remedy —* ***the remedy is not to leave a gate red, it is to make the declaration AUDITABLE***: *the gap is now declared as **register IDs**, checkable against `THE_LIVE_ARC` by anyone who doubts the green.* | r2445 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ INCONSISTENCY AND OVERREACH ARE OPPOSITE SIGNATURES, AND NO INSTRUMENT SEES BOTH.** *`L-230` named a class — **a point result written as a family result** — and `scope_table` was adopted and sharpened to catch it. **A blind run across all seventeen papers found every flag in P15 and NOTHING in P14, the class's clearest instance.*** ⇒ **The reason is structural, not a tuning failure:** *`scope_table` measures **disagreement between receipts**, and `F14`'s defect is **agreement*** — *every P14 receipt runs at $M=0.12$ and **the paper generalises from that single value**.* ⇒ ***INCONSISTENCY IS MANY VALUES WHERE ONE IS CLAIMED; OVERREACH IS ONE VALUE WHERE MANY ARE CLAIMED. An instrument tuned to the first is blind to the second by construction, and no amount of sharpening crosses that.*** ⌗ **AND THE GENERAL FORM IS WORTH MORE THAN THE INSTANCE:** *before adopting an instrument for a class, **ask what the class's SIGNATURE is in the data the instrument reads**. Here the class's signature was uniformity, and the tool was a variance detector.* ⌗ ⛭ **AND THE ROUTE THAT REMAINS IS THE ONE THE CORPUS ALREADY KNOWS:** *detecting overreach means comparing a receipt's scope against **the sentence's quantifier**, which is reading a claim and therefore a **judgement** —* ***and a gate can check a DECLARATION, not a judgement.*** *So the answer is a **convention** like `\rcpt{}`: the `COMPUTES:` header, which **39 of 309 receipts already carry**, and which `P14_leaf_compactness` uses to state exactly the scope its citing sentence drops.* ⇒ ***Twice now the answer to "what instrument sees this?" has been "none — and the corpus already invented the convention that would."*** | r2447 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ A STALE MARKER AND A MISSED LANDING LOOK IDENTICAL FROM OUTSIDE THE FILE — and fourteen of twenty-four were the second.** *`check_currency` had never been green. The natural reading of twenty-four documents sitting 22 revisions behind is **bookkeeping**: bump the markers, move on.* ⇒ ⛔ ***Fourteen of them had missed something real, and each failed at its own stated job:*** *a **lensing ledger** that did not know lensing had been built; a file saying **"read this before touching P13, P14"** that knew neither of the sector's two newest results; a file saying **"read this to know the position"** that did not know the programme had moved into a repository; **"the grid, which paper × which tool"** listing neither of the two tools just adopted.* ⌗ **THE RULE: `check_currency` does not measure tidiness. It measures whether a document still does its job** *— so **the marker is the symptom and the job is the test**, and the only way to tell them apart is to **open the file and ask what it claims to be for**.* ⚠ ***AND THE DECLARATION OF NO-EFFECT IS A CLAIM AND MUST BE ARGUED:*** *the last seven were declared current only after the fourteen beside them proved that "probably nothing moved" is a bad prior.* ⌗⌗ **AND THE CONDUCT NOTE, because it is instances SEVEN, EIGHT AND NINE:** *this line reached for **word-count probes three times in one sweep** — grepping "leaf", "deck", "lensing" across the stale set to decide which mattered.* ***Every document "hit", because those are core CR vocabulary. Word-presence dressed as relevance, again.*** ⇒ ***What worked, all three times, was READING ONE DOCUMENT instead of counting across twenty*** *— and what is worth recording is not that the probes failed but that* **this line keeps picking up the same broken tool after filing the rule against it six times.** | r2449 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ A STRING HAS NO POLARITY — instances TEN and ELEVEN, and they failed in OPPOSITE directions on one pass.** *Auditing thirty routed items against the current tree, this line wrote a presence-test per item and read the output as a verdict.* ⛔ **Item 14** *asked the fork to ADD the words "along that axis", so **the fix's presence read as "still live"**.* ⛔ **Item 17** *asked the fork to REPLACE an unqualified phrase, and the paper now says* **"The unqualified form of this clause … is withdrawn"** *— so* ***the WITHDRAWAL, which quotes the phrase in order to retract it, read as "still live" too.*** ⇒ ***One test, two rows, two opposite polarity errors, and both would have re-routed work already done.*** ⌗ **THE RULE: a string is evidence that words are present, never that a claim is true or false — so before believing a presence-test, state what a HIT would mean and what a MISS would mean, per row.** *Here that is one sentence per item and it costs nothing.* ⌗⌗ ***AND THIS IS THE SAME ROOT AS THE NINE BEFORE IT*** *(`G1`'s distance, the phrase test, the quotation check, the coverage ratio, the label-mention count, a defined function read for its behaviour, and three word-presence probes in the currency sweep):* **taking an instrument's surface for its behaviour.** ⇒ ***Eleven instances now, and the honest summary is not "be careful with greps" but: THIS LINE REACHES FOR A COUNTABLE PROXY EVERY TIME A JUDGEMENT IS DUE ACROSS MORE THAN ~5 ITEMS, and the proxy is wrong roughly as often as it is right.*** *What has worked, without exception, is **opening one instance and reading it**.* | r2452 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ A METRIC OVER AN OPEN-ENDED SET OF REALISATIONS MEASURES THE METRIC-WRITER'S IMAGINATION — the twelfth and thirteenth instances, and the first to CLOSE a row rather than merely embarrass.** *`L-220` asked for a rebuilt quantity for "can a reader see a claim's support?". The successor was the right SHAPE — **a declaration binary, no invented threshold** — and it converged instead of settling:* **20 flags on two routes → 7 with the proof environment → 4 once two of the seven were READ and turned up a bracketed `[Established: …]` tag and a following `\noindent\emph{Argument.}` paragraph.** ⇒ ***Every sharpening lowered the count and every flag read was a route the instrument did not know. Nothing bounds the ways prose can supply an arrival path.*** ⌗ **THE RULE, and it is sharper than "beware proxies":** ***before instrumenting a class, ask whether its realisations are ENUMERABLE. If a thing can be done in indefinitely many legitimate ways, a detector for it measures the detector, and its flag count is a report on the author's coverage.*** ⌗ *Contrast the cases where instrumentation DID work — `check_citations`, `check_id_bands` — **both check a DECLARATION with exactly one legitimate form**. That is the discriminator: **one form ⇒ gateable; open-ended ⇒ readable only.*** ⛭⛭ **AND THE CLASS DISSOLVED INTO A FACT ABOUT READERS:** *`L-220`'s three founding instances — the answer two sections above the question, P16 answering in the next sentence, `rem:carter-chain` already written — **all had arrival paths that a reader missed**.* ⇒ ***All three were filed as corpus defects and all three resolved into the corpus having supplied the path. A node that cannot find support for a claim should file it as a report about its own reading.*** | r2453 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ FOLLOW A FREEDOM TO THE END BEFORE CALLING IT SPARE — and the seven-run record of `L-211` is the argument.** *At r2458 this line named $E(r)$'s profile **"an unspent degree of freedom"**, available to the progenitor route. **At r2460, following it to the end, it turned out the corpus had already spent it:** a varying $E(r)$ is a growing curvature mode, and* ***the capstone fixes its spread by running the transfer chain backwards from the observed amplitude.*** ⇒ ***THE FREE FUNCTION AND THE INHERITED DATUM WERE THE SAME FREEDOM SEEN FROM TWO ENDS.*** ⌗ **THE RULE:** *a degree of freedom is not spare because you can NAME it; it is spare only when you have followed **every** place the corpus could be constraining it and found none.* ***Counting parameters is a proxy; tracing them is a reading*** *— which is the same discriminator as the thirteen bad-measure instances, arriving in a physics argument rather than in an instrument.* ⛭ **AND WHAT SURVIVED WAS STRONGER THAN THE CLAIM IT REPLACED:** *the SIGN of $E$ is forced by nucleosynthesis and its PROFILE SCALE by measurement, so* ***neither half is an undeclared free parameter*** *— a better statement about the construction than the one being corrected.* ⌗⌗ ***AND THIS IS WHY A TRIGGERED FRONT BEATS AN ENUMERATED ONE: seven runs produced a withdrawal, a relocated frontier, a collapsed three-item debt, two narrowings of a protected question, an unstated premise, and a correction of this line's own claim two revisions old. An enumerated list cannot produce the last one, because it does not revisit.*** | r2460 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ CHECK THE DEFECT, NOT JUST THE REPORT — the mirror of "check the fix, not just the diagnosis", and it stopped a build.** *The fork routed a real defect it had found and repaired on its own tree: sixteen lines of P15 prose standing twice, with the copy glued to the next paragraph so a demonstrative pointed at the wrong object. **This line accepted it and promised a scanner in a routed file.*** ⇒ ***Scanning every commit that ever touched the file, across all refs, at paragraph / sentence / line-window granularity: ZERO duplicates, everywhere — because the two commits added DIFFERENT prose, two paraphrases of one finding, so git flagged it and it was resolved by hand.*** **The duplicate was in the fork's tree and never reached `main`.** ⌗ **THE RULE: a routed report is a claim about a TREE, and trees differ. Reproduce the defect on YOUR tree before you build against it** *— the same discipline as r2417's "a receipt naming a defect is evidence it was FOUND, never that it is still THERE", now pointed at a report from the other line rather than at one's own past.* ⚠ **AND THE PROMISE WAS KEPT BY BEING WITHDRAWN WITH ITS REASON:** ***a detector that cannot be shown catching its own motivating case prints "clean" for an unknown reason*** *— `check_loci`'s shipped `strip('\b')` lesson and `L-220`'s converging route-sets, both saying the same thing.* ⇒ ***An unbuilt instrument with a stated reason beats a built one validated against nothing, and an unkept promise in a routing channel is worse than either — so it is withdrawn IN the channel, not silently.*** ⌗ *What survives is the mechanism the fork routed rather than the instance: **step 6 asks about FILES, and a prose duplicate inside one file is invisible to it.** That gap is real, is recorded, and waits for an exhibit.* | r2463 | LIVE | never |

| **⛔⛔ A MARKER CONSTRAINS AN ACT, NEVER A SUBJECT — and reading `PROTECTED_OPEN` as "do not touch" took four live research questions off the board silently.** *At r2463 this line reported PO-5, PO-6, PO-9 and `L-202` as **"gated or protected"** and set them aside.* ⇒ ***`PROTECTED_OPEN`'s own header says the opposite: "The register of open research questions a node may NOT CLOSE … **a node may write a bounded negative; a closure on a protected item is unseated.**"*** ⌗ **AND THE DOCUMENT'S OWN REASONING MAKES THE MISREADING WORSE, not better:** *it exists because "every mechanical instrument this corpus has ever built polices OVER-CLAIMING … **NOTHING polices a NEGATIVE verdict on an open question** … the guard set is not merely silent there — **it LEANS**."* ⇒ ***It was written because tired nodes KILL things. Reading it as "don't engage" is the same failure wearing the opposite coat — and it costs MORE, because a premature closure is at least visible in the register while a silent set-aside is not.*** ⌗⌗ **AND THE SAME INVERSION RAN THROUGH EVERY "unseated" ROW:** *"unseated on **whether** P3 is reordered" became "don't work P3"; "each **rung** of the ladder is unseated" became "don't build the formats or the SPEC" — **when `L-218`'s own next step reads "the companion SPEC written as a repo document before any code"**, which is this line's to write.* ⇒ ***One row on the board was genuinely held and said so in its own words ("⏸ HELD BY DARYL r2381 — deliberately"). Everything else marked "his" was this line converting "Daryl decides X" into "Daryl must do everything adjacent to X".*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***read which ACT a marker names. "May not close" permits every other verb. "Decides whether" governs the decision and not the preparation.*** ⌗ *And the yield of finally working one: `L-221` turned out to have **two of its three factual premises stale** — the corpus had gained a specified 12/3 split, a particle-level baryon, and a colourless grading count of four — **and nothing was looking, because a protected row had been read as untouchable.*** | r2464 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ LOCATE THE BASE BEFORE YOU ASK WHAT VARIES OVER IT — and consulting the instrument first is what found the error.** *At r2467 this line derived that a curvature source for the flat colour bundle "would have to be a spatially varying $m(r)$", on the reasoning that **the branch points move with $M$** (true) and therefore that **the flatness is forced by the homogeneity of the leaf** (false).* ⇒ ⛔ ***P14 carries "every root returns the SAME $2M=r_0-r_0^3$, so the three carry ONE mass parameter": the base is the $2M$-PLANE and the fibre is the three roots. The branch points sit OVER the $2M$-plane, not ON the leaf.*** ⇒⇒ ***A covering map carries a canonical FLAT connection by definition, so the flatness is what it MEANS for the module to be a branching — and a varying $m(r)$ merely MOVES THE BASE POINT, under which a cover stays flat. There was nothing to compute.*** ⌗ **THE RULE:** ***before asking what could vary over a bundle, establish what its BASE is. "The branch points move with $M$" and "the branch points move across the leaf" are different sentences, and only the first is true.*** ⛭⛭ **AND THE METHOD POINT IS THE ONE TO CARRY:** *the row's own instruction was **"consult the discriminant BEFORE computing"**.* ***Consulting it sent this line back to the structure, and the structure held the error.
