| **⛭⛭ "I DON'T WANT TO GUESS ITS PRECISION" IS A CORRECT REFUSAL, AND THE COMPLETION OF IT IS TO MEASURE THE PRECISION.** *`L-514` asked for a check that a withdrawal paragraph does not contradict text in its own section. 54 declined to build it, saying it did not want to guess how precise the signature would be — **and left the row live rather than shipping a lint it could not calibrate**.* ⇒ ***Measured rather than guessed: **signature A** (a withdrawal sharing a number with its section) fires 10 times and is the NORMAL case — a withdrawal restating the number it withdraws is what an honest one does; **signature B** (a bound "and no further", then a larger value) fires 20 times, catches the real one FIRST, and the other nineteen are coincidences of magnitude.*** ⇒⇒ ***~1 real in 20 — not a precision worth shipping, because a gate at that rate teaches nodes to ignore it, which costs more than the defect it catches.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***"the precision is bad" is a FINDING. "I didn't build it" is not.*** *The refusal was right and is now backed by a number instead of an instinct — and the row closes on the measurement rather than staying live forever on an unresolved instinct.* ⌗ **AND THE INSTANCE ITSELF WAS ALREADY DISCHARGED IN THE CORPUS:** *P15 now carries the over-withdrawal and its correction **in sequence in the same section**, ending **"That reading was too wide, and the correction is entered here."*** ⇒ ***The paper kept both, which is exactly what the row wanted and nobody had gone back to look.*** | r2553 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ AN EXTENSION LEFT UNAPPLIED FOR A HUNDRED REVISIONS WAS NOT EXPENSIVE — IT WAS WRONG, AND THE GRAMMAR SAID SO.** *`L-228` asked to extend `check_loci` to **possessive and compound-noun** forms. Node 52 built it, **left it unapplied, and declined to claim a precision for it** — and nobody since could say why.* ⇒ **⓵ Measured: **25 possessive and 73 compound sites**, which reads as a large change. ⓶ But the gate reads **receipt-bound sentences only**, and just **8 of the 98** are bound** — *so the cost objection was never the real one.* ⇒ ⛭ **⓷ AND THE GRAMMAR SETTLED IT:** *the `ASSERT` patterns require the verb to follow the locus, and a possessive puts a noun in between —* ***"the branch point's RADIATION AMPLITUDE takes …" asserts about the amplitude, not about the branch point.*** ⇒⇒ ***A possessive is a MODIFIER, not a subject; "branch-point crossing" names the CROSSING. Neither form is a locus claim, so neither belongs in a gate that checks locus claims.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***when an instrument change has sat unapplied for a long time and nobody can say why, the reason is often that it is WRONG rather than expensive — and the way to find out is to build it and look at what it matches.*** *Three measurements, in order: the site count said "large", the bound said "eight", and the grammar said "none of them".* ⚠ *And the distinction that had to be kept: **this was never the recall gap 54 named** — items 19 and 20 were found by reading because they are **not receipt-bound at all**. ***This widens the FORMS inside the bound; that gap is the BOUND. Two different things, and conflating them would have justified the wrong build.*** ⌗ *Reverted after being run, with **the reasoning written into `check_loci.py` itself** — so the next node finds it where the attempt would be made, not in a changelog.* | r2554 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ A LOADER THAT SILENTLY DISCARDS INPUT IS A CLASS, AND ITS TELL IS "SOMETHING WAS WRITTEN AND DID NOT ARRIVE."** *c54.206 named it from three instances and said a fourth would warrant a gate.* ⇒ ***There were six.*** *· `check_receipts` filtering INDEX rows on `startswith('| P')` while the geometric core is **`p0` lowercase** — nine receipts invisible; · `make_receipt_appendix` with the same filter — **the same nine dropped from the printed reproducibility layer, and an uncited receipt that vanishes leaves no dead link**; · `regen_board`'s LEADS dict with **two `L-171` keys**, silent, later wins; · `check_currency`'s regex rejecting the compound `rNNNN+c54.N` — **40 of 70 declarations never parsed**; · `run_all_receipts` and `work_entry_points` carrying the first filter unfixed — **twelve receipts the runner never ran** — plus a **SECOND load site inside `check_receipts` itself**, left behind when the first was corrected; · and **`check_supersession`, found by the new gate on its FIRST RUN**.* ⇒ ⛭ **THE SHAPE:** ***the input is well-formed, the loader rejects it on a convention it does not know, and NOTHING REPORTS THE REJECTION — so the gate downstream reports a consequence whose cause is unreachable from what it reads.*** ⌗ **AND TWO SUB-RULES EARNED IN THE BUILDING:** *· ***one fix per FILE is not one fix per LOAD SITE*** — a file that loads the same table twice can be half-fixed with nothing to show it; · ***a lint that cannot tell a quoted mention from a call punishes documentation*** — the first version flagged `make_receipt_appendix`'s own **docstring account of the c54.36 fix** as a live filter, which is exactly backwards. **Tokenising, not string tests.*** ⚠ **AND THE LIMIT IS THE HONEST HALF:** ***a silent discard is by construction invisible, so a gate for it can only encode the shapes already caught. The instance list is the SPECIFICATION, not a defect record*** *— which is c54.206's own namespace-lint property, one register out.* | r2555 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭ A VEIN CAN GO STALE IN TWO MOODS, AND ONLY ONE OF THEM IS GATEABLE.** *r2538 caught `PO-6` **stating a claim its leads had retracted**. r2556 caught `PO-9` **asking a question its lead had answered** — "whether the CUT's four-ness carries the FORCING of the dynamics", four revisions after `L-240` closed with **"it does"**.* ⇒ ***Same cost from opposite directions: a vein's DARK half is a list of what is NOT KNOWN, and a finished question on it sends the next node to dig where the digging is done.*** ⌗ **AND THE GENERAL CHECK WAS BUILT, RUN, AND NOT SHIPPED.** *Signature: a vein clause naming a STRUCK row beside an interrogative. With the real instance fixed it reported **two hits, both legitimate** — one clause **recording a correction**, one **citing a closed row for what it settled while asking what remains**.* ⇒ ***0 real in 2. Third time this span that a lint was built, measured, and rejected on its own numbers (`L-514` r2553, `L-228` r2554, this).*** ⛭ **AND THE PATTERN ACROSS THE THREE IS WORTH MORE THAN ANY ONE:** ***the retraction table works because it names SPECIFIC withdrawn claims; every general form of "detect a stale idea" has failed on precision.*** ⇒ *So the instrument for this class is not a lint at all — it is the **closure-neighbour report** (r2542): when a lead closes, **read the veins it fed**. ***That says WHERE to look without pretending to judge; the lint would have judged and been wrong.*** | r2556 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ A CONVENTION WITH FALLING UPTAKE HAD THE WRONG TARGET, AND THE FIX WAS TO SHRINK THE POPULATION, NOT TO PUSH HARDER.** *`L-230`'s route said **"make `COMPUTES:` universal"** — and the share had fallen 12.6% → 11.2% → **10.1%** while the corpus grew.* ⇒ ⛔ ***But ~85% of receipts name no concrete parameter at all: a universal check would demand a scope declaration from receipts that HAVE no scope, which is why nobody complied and why nobody could say what was wrong.*** ⌗ **THE HEADER EXISTS FOR ONE CLASS** *— a receipt running at one parameter while the citing sentence means another —* ***and that class only exists for receipts that RUN at a parameter. Population: 102, not 397.*** ⛭ **AND THEN IT SHIPPED GRANDFATHERED, which is the whole design:** *91 failures on day one is a gate nobody runs.* ⇒ ***The 91 freeze into a baseline of PATHS — a COUNT would let a new offender hide behind a removed one — and anything added since must declare. The numerator grows with the denominator, which is the only thing that turns a falling share around.*** ⚠ **AND THE PRIOR CLOSURE WAS CORRECTED RATHER THAN OVERTURNED:** *r2447 closed this because **"no gate can read a quantifier"** — ***true of the header's TRUTH, false of its PRESENCE***. A closure about the unreachable half had left the reachable half unaddressed for a hundred revisions.* ⌗ **THE RULE:** ***when a convention is not being adopted, check whether it is asking the wrong population before concluding it is being ignored.*** *And when it is: **grandfather the past and gate the future** — a rule that makes 91 existing things fail is not a rule, it is a wish.* | r2557 | LIVE | never |

| **⛔⛔⛭ A WORD-BOUNDED COUNT IS THE RIGHT FIRST INSTRUMENT, AND THIS IS WHERE IT GIVES THE WRONG ANSWER.** *`VARIATIONAL_LEDGER.md` opened at r1901 titled **"the field with no footprint at all"**, from the r1890 hole survey, with Daryl's adjudication attached. **It stood 657 revisions.*** ⇒ ⛔ ***The premise is false. `Lagrangian` 0, `action principle` 0, `Euler–Lagrange` 0 — but the EINSTEIN–HILBERT ACTION appears FOUR times and every one is load-bearing: the ADM starting point; an objection answered on it ("the conformal factor entering the EH action with the opposite sign … that objection does not reach this construction"); a Hamiltonian reduced from it; and*** ⛭ ***a SECOND-ORDER expansion in the transverse-traceless sector "reduced, mode by mode, to that of a harmonic oscillator".*** ⇒⇒ ***That last is variational work PERFORMED in the corpus, with the field named nowhere.*** ⌗ **AND IT IS THE ARRIVAL-PATH SHAPE AT ITS LARGEST SCALE:** *the six earlier instances were **a missing name beside a held ARGUMENT**; this is* ***a missing name beside a performed COMPUTATION.*** ⛭ **THE RULE, and it is about instruments rather than physics:** ***a hole survey by word count finds the fields the corpus does not USE. It cannot find the fields the corpus uses UNDER ANOTHER NAME — and those are exactly the ones where the corpus is doing the work and failing to say so.*** ⚠ *The r1890 survey was not careless: **it was reading the only signal it had**, and the fix is to check the named OBJECTS a field owns (here: the action itself) before writing "zero".* ⌗ *And the ledger's title now says what is true, with the original reading kept beside it — **rewriting a record to look better is a different failure.*** | r2558 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ THE KILL RECEIPT'S VALUE WAS THE CHECK IT FAILED, AND SEVEN REVISIONS LATER THAT CHECK CLEARED.** *`kills/PO-7.md` (r2552) was owed for fifty revisions while this line asked for a conversion instead of writing it. Written, **① ⑤ passed, ③ found the prices asymmetric, and ④ CHAIN DID NOT CLEAR**: the 0.408 rested on four things reproduced twice on two instrument paths and on **P15's freezing argument — an ARGUMENT, reproduced nowhere**.* ⇒ ***That failure WAS the finding. It narrowed `PO-7` from "is the disagreement real against the sky?" to "does every mode of interest freeze before the crossing?" — and named a calculation.*** ⌗ ✔ **cc54 RAN IT:** *every multipole **28–2475** freezes strictly before the crossing (88% of the leg down to **0.065%**, the thinnest margin still frozen), the sub-horizon ratio **$\to0$ at the crossing for all of them** (max $5.2\times10^{-4}$), and* ***the inversion is closed by the divergence of $|aH|$ — no mode crosses unfrozen, the band stays 0.2069, the control is outside it.*** ⇒⇒ ***All five links now reproduced on two paths; ④ clears.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***a check that fails is worth more than four that pass, because it says what to compute. A kill receipt is not a formality to be filled in — it is an instrument for finding which link is weakest, and its failing check is its output.*** ⚠ **AND THE PROCEDURE'S OWN LIMIT HELD ON BOTH SIDES:** *cc54 supplied **the calculation, not the verdict**, saying so; this line updated the receipt and **did not convert the row**. ⇒ ***`PROTECTED_OPEN` says "and Daryl authorises", and that is the one place in this programme where asking is what the register asks for — which is a different thing from manufacturing a decision, and the difference is that the register wrote it down first.*** | r2559 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭ QUEUED WORK BELONGS ON THE THING THAT GATES IT, NOT ON A LEAD LIST.** *`L-207` — P8's **"the deepest open question the construction raises"** — had **⓵ discharged at r2450** and **⓶ reading "nothing until `PO-6` moves"**.* ⇒ ***A row whose only remaining half is "wait" is not a lead.*** ⌗ **AND THE DISTINCTION FROM `L-217` (r2553) IS THE POINT:** *there, `F1` was a **standing trip-wire** and nothing becomes available when it holds — so the row simply closed. Here **⓶ is real work that becomes available the moment `PO-6` moves**.* ⇒ ***So it is not discarded but RELOCATED: carried on `PO-6`'s vein as "what unlocks when this moves", where the node working that vein will read it — instead of sitting on a lead list telling every node to wait.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***a gated task on a lead list is an instruction to do nothing, addressed to everyone. On the gating vein it is an instruction to do something, addressed to whoever gets there.*** *Same information, and the placement is the whole difference.* ⌗ *And the same reading struck `L-805` in the same turn: cc54's freezing calculation is **absorbed into `kills/PO-7.md`'s ④** and its remainder is **`F5`'s procedure** — a standing condition, not a task.* ⇒ ***A row whose work is absorbed and whose remainder is a procedure is a RECORD.*** ⚠ *Neither strike claims progress: **`PO-6`'s dark half is unchanged**, and B·2 is no nearer than it was. **Relocation is not closure, and saying which one happened is the whole of the honesty here.*** | r2560 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ A THREE-WAY HONESTY RULE IS IN PRACTICE A FOUR-WAY ONE, AND THE FOURTH BUCKET IS THE DEFAULT.** *`L-218`'s companion rule: **every claim is ESTABLISHED (cite the receipt), OPEN (cite `PROTECTED_OPEN`), or DO-NOT-ASSERT (say why)** — "enforceable rather than aspirational".* ⇒ *Measured: ***ESTABLISHED 308 receipts · OPEN 9 items · DO-NOT-ASSERT 16 phrases = 333 anchors, against 7480 sentences of substance.**** ⇒⇒ ***So the honest default for everything unanchored is "the corpus DISCUSSES this" — and that covers the overwhelming majority of what anyone will ask.*** ⌗ **AND THAT IS THE RULE WORKING:** *the usual companion's failure is **answering confidently about discussion**; this one must say which mode it is in.* ⇒ ***The price of enforceability is saying "discussed, not established" most of the time — and naming the fourth state is what keeps it honest rather than SILENT, because a companion with no legal answer to an unanchored question will invent one.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***when a scheme sorts claims into N states, check what fraction of the material falls outside all N. If it is most of it, the scheme has an unnamed default — and an unnamed default is where the dishonesty goes.*** ⚠ **AND ONE MEASUREMENT WAS DISCARDED RATHER THAN REPORTED:** *a first attempt scored **1360 `\emph{}` passages** against **104 nearby `\rcpt{}`** and produced **"8% coverage"**.* ⇒ ***Meaningless — `\emph{}` is the corpus's EMPHASIS voice, not a claim marker, so the denominator counted every emphasised phrase in seventeen papers. A wrong denominator produces a number that looks like a finding, and the only defence is asking what the denominator IS before dividing.*** | r2561 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ BUILD THE LINT, MEASURE IT, AND USUALLY DON'T SHIP IT — the sixth mechanism, and it earned itself three times in nine revisions.** *`L-514` (a withdrawal contradicting its own section): **~1 real in 20**. `L-228` (possessive locus forms): **8 sites, and the GRAMMAR says none of them are locus claims**. `L-175` (a vein asking a question its lead answered): **0 real in 2**. **None shipped.*** ⇒ ⛭ **AND THE PATTERN ACROSS THE THREE IS THE ENTRY:** ***the checks that DO work name SPECIFIC things — `check_veins`' retraction table, `check_computes`' baseline of paths, `check_loaders`' instance list. Every GENERAL form of "detect a stale idea" has failed on precision.*** ⌗ **SO WHY BUILD THEM AT ALL:** *each produced a real finding ***in the building*** — `L-514`'s instance was **already fixed in P15**; `L-228`'s grammar showed the extension was **wrong rather than expensive**, which a hundred revisions of deferral had never established; `L-175`'s single true positive was **a live defect on a vein**.* ⇒ ***The lint was the INSTRUMENT, not the deliverable.*** ⚠ **AND THE COMPLEMENT IS WHERE THE CLASS ACTUALLY GETS CAUGHT:** *the **closure-neighbour report** — when a lead closes, read the veins it fed.* ⇒ ***It says WHERE to look without pretending to judge, and all three rejected lints were trying to do the judging part.*** ⌗⌗ **AND A SIBLING TO THE JOINS PATTERN EMERGED IN THE SAME SPAN:** *`L-230` closed because **its route asked the wrong POPULATION**; `VARIATIONAL_LEDGER`'s title was **false for 657 revisions**.* ⇒ ***A long-open row is often not a join OR a gap but a MIS-STATED QUESTION — and re-measuring the question is cheaper than working it.*** | r2562 | LIVE | never |

The one physical length is $\alpha$, not $\ell_P$; their ratio $\sim10^{61}$ … the size of the universe in gauge-units — A NUMBER, NOT A TUNING."*** ⌗ *And "cross-register" is p0's own word: the Planck values **mix the thermal $\hbar$ with the real-geometric $c$ and $G$**.* ⇒ ***So $T$ is a one-register quantity and $S$ is a gauge-count across two — a STRUCTURAL reason for the asymmetry, and the ratio is the one-constant claim's OWN CONSEQUENCE rather than a threat to it.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***a zero-count tells you a word is absent. It does not tell you the CONTENT is absent — and when the count is right, the next question is not "why is this missing?" but "under what other name is it already here?"*** ⚠ *This is the same failure as `VARIATIONAL_LEDGER`'s at r2558, one register in: **there a word count read content as absent; here a word count read content as UNRECONCILED**. | **⛔⛭ A `kind:` THE CLASSIFIER DID NOT KNOW TOOK DOWN EVERY GATE AT ONCE — the silent-discard class's loud cousin.** *Two new PLAN documents were written with `kind: PLAN`. `classify_documents` indexes an `order` dict **by hard subscript**, and `PLAN` was not in it.* ⇒ ⛔ ***KeyError — and because every gate reads `INDEX.md`, which that script generates, EIGHT gates went red at once on well-formed input.*** ⌗ **AND THE SHAPE IS r2555's, INVERTED:** *there, a loader **silently discarded** input and the gate downstream reported a consequence whose cause was invisible. Here the loader ***crashed loudly*** — ***which is strictly better, because the failure named its own file and line.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***a loader meeting an unknown convention should fail SOFT on that entry, not hard on the run. `order.get(k, 99)` sorts an unknown kind last; `order[k]` takes down the corpus.*** ⚠ *And the deeper point is the same as the namespace-lint property c54.206 named: **the conventions live in the corpus, not in the rule** — so any loader keyed to an enumerated set will meet a member it does not know, and the only question is whether that day is a one-line fix or a red board.* ⌗ *Registered `PLAN` **and** made the sort tolerant — **both**, because registering the one known case is what the previous five instances of this class each did, and each was followed by a sixth.* | r2566 | LIVE | never |

And three are CHECKS against work already done, so a candidate can be tested before anything new is computed.*** ⛭⛭ **THE RULE:** ***when a question reads as open-ended, ask what the corpus already FORBIDS before asking what it permits. The graph is near-complete and has no source, so no topological order exists AND NONE CAN.*** ⌗ **SO "THE BOOK IN EVERY READING FORMAT" IS UNDEFINED AT ITS FIRST STEP** *— and `COMPANION_SPEC` says so by omission: **`order` zero times, `sequence` zero times**.* ⇒ *A14 in order: **(1) decide what "the book" IS · (2) a front-matter note that the corpus is a NETWORK, with the centrality order as an approach and the forward references named as unavoidable · (3) then pandoc, which IS an afternoon.*** ***Step 3 was the whole of A14 as written.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***a task called "just the plumbing" should still get one measurement before it is scheduled, because the plumbing's INPUT may not exist. | **⛔⛔⛭ A `%` COMMENT BECAME A QUOTATION AND NEARLY BECAME A PAPER CLAIM — and nothing in the tree measured the first step.** *c54.207: a header comment in P16 carried "peak spacing"; a receipt's docstring quoted the surrounding sentence **in quotation marks** as "P16 distinguishes it explicitly"; and the paragraph being drafted was about to cite it.* ⇒ ***Measured: `peak spacing` is **0 in P16's body, 1 in P16's comments, 6 in P15's body**. The claim was true of P15, attributed to P16, and its source was a comment nobody treats as text.*** ⌗ **AND THE REASON NOTHING CAUGHT IT IS STRUCTURAL:** *every provenance check here asserts against a paper's **rendered content** — `check_loci`, `check_receipt_asserts`, `check_citations`.* ⇒ ***A comment is not rendered, so it is invisible to all of them — and a quotation lifted from one carries the authority of the paper it sits in while being no part of it.*** ⛭ **THE HOP IS THE POINT:** ***header comment → a receipt's docstring, where it READS as a quotation → a paper. Each step is individually innocent, and the authority is manufactured entirely by the second.*** ✔ *`check_provenance` built and wired; **population ZERO**, since 54 fixed the one instance and no other exists across 400-odd receipts.* ⇒ ***Which is exactly when a gate is cheap: it costs nothing today and forbids the path that was walked once.*** ⚠ *And its limit is stated rather than discovered: **it cannot catch the REVERSE hop** — a claim invented in a docstring and later quoted as though it came from a paper. **Same class, out of reach.*** | r2572 | LIVE | never |

A citation loop between two papers written together is invisible to it — and it is a property of the PAIR, not a mistake in either alone.*** ⚠ *And the discharge is one clause: **one paper derives the triple, or P12 states it as its own** — which paper owns it is authorial.* ⌗ ***This is item 45's class (`Teitelboim1973` cited for FORM where the CONTENT is elsewhere), and the second instance is what makes it a class rather than a slip.*** | r2575 | LIVE | never |

And `PO-6` — **269 characters** — after **the quartic was answered, the Nariai expansion parameter named, the one-constant obstacle removed, and the sum given seven necessary conditions**.* ⇒ ⛔ ***The register had been told none of it.*** ⌗ **AND THE MECHANISM IS ORDINARY, WHICH IS WHY IT PERSISTED:** *findings were written to the **veins** (`BOARD.md`, generated), to the **register rows** (`THE_LIVE_ARC`), and to the **changelog** — all three of which a node reads. **`PROTECTED_OPEN` is read when something is CLOSING, not when something is progressing**, so it accumulated nothing while everything else accumulated everything.* ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***a document read only at one moment in a lifecycle goes stale between those moments, and the staler it is the less anyone consults it — which is the loop. Twenty-four of the twenty-eight were never work.*** ⌗ **AND THE REAL TOTAL IS TWELVE**, *sorted by the only axis that matters: **6 calculations · 3 authorisations · 2 paragraphs · 1 editorial.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***a heading count measures FORMAT, not obligation. Read `kind:` before counting — a RECORD's unmarked heading means "this was reported", and a QUEUE's means "this is owed", and nothing in the text distinguishes them.*** ⚠ *And the framing that produced the better answer was not "how many items" but **"what KIND OF ACT finishes each"** — because that axis is stable under miscounting: ***the six calculations were six whether the total was 12 or 28.*** ⌗ *Filed with the miscount named in the file it was written into, because **a count that was wrong once will be trusted next time unless the correction sits where the counting happens**.* | r2577 | LIVE | never |

| **⛔⛭⛭ A COMPLETENESS CLAIM THAT IS NOT TESTED AGAINST THE SOURCE IS A GUESS, AND THIS ONE WAS WRONG BY TWO.** *`THE_STATE` (r2577) said twelve unfinished things and implied that was everything. Daryl asked whether it covered **the theatres and the papers' own frontier sections**.* ⇒ ⛔ ***It had not been checked. Tested: **18 frontier/open sections across 13 papers**.*** ⌗ **AND P7 KEEPS ITS OWN COUNT HONESTLY, WHICH IS WHAT MADE THE GAP FINDABLE:** *"**it opened at seven and stands at four**", with items one, three and seven **resolved and moved below the list** — and the paper says why: **"a frontier list whose entries quietly empty is worse than none: a reader uses it to choose what to work."*** ⇒ *Of the four remaining, `PROTECTED_OPEN` had taken up **three**.* ⇒⇒ ⛔ ***The fourth — the SCALAR PERTURBATION SECTOR's stated remainder — was in NO register: not `PROTECTED_OPEN`, not the arc, not `THE_STATE`. And it is TWO named runs in the paper's own words: the full-spectrum likelihood comparison against flat $\Lambda$CDM, and the odd/even height pattern.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***a register takes up frontier items ONE AT A TIME, and nothing re-reads the source section afterward. So a paper that maintains its own list correctly can still have an item that no register ever collected — and the corpus's completeness is only ever as good as its last sweep of the SOURCES.*** ⚠ *And it is r2576's lesson one register out: **`PROTECTED_OPEN` goes stale between closures; the papers' frontier sections go stale between REGISTRATIONS**.* ⌗ ***Not a defect in P7: the paper says what it owes, in its own voice, and even says the bulk of the item is settled. The defect is that nobody read it back.*** | r2578 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ A PAPER THAT RESOLVES A FRONTIER ITEM AND LEAVES THE RESOLUTION IN THE FRONTIER SECTION HAS WRITTEN A CHANGELOG, NOT A RESULT.** *Daryl asked whether crossed-off items get **shifted into the synthesis and physics sections, "as if they were written when the final product had already been sorted."*** ⇒ **Tested.** *P7 says three items were **"moved below the list"** and **"recorded there"** —* ⛭ ***but `\section{Frontiers and open problems}` is P7's LAST section, so "below the list" means below the enumerate INSIDE it.*** ⌗ **MEASURED:** *P7's frontier section is **32,368 chars, 11% of its body**, and **35% of that sits below the list** — roughly **eleven thousand characters of settled physics under a heading that says it is not done**.* ⇒ ⌗ **AND THE CONTENT IS PRESENT ELSEWHERE TOO** *(`causal reassignment` **21 times** in P7).* ⇒⇒ ***So the defect is PLACEMENT, not absence — and that is worse in one specific way: the same result appears both as worked physics and as a resolved frontier, so a reader meeting it first in the frontier section reads settled work as an open edge.*** ⛭⛭ **CORPUS-WIDE: 191,530 characters of frontier/open sections across thirteen papers** — *`boundary_paper` **33% of its body**, `CR_cosmology` **29%**.* ⚠ **AND THE HONEST LIMIT:** *how much of that is RESOLVED is **not** established — the resolved-language proxy finds **20 markers**, far too few to count with, and was reported as a proxy rather than a number.* ⇒ ***So the deliverable is an AUDIT, not a conclusion: for each of the thirteen sections, which paragraphs state an OPEN edge and which a RESOLVED one.*** ⛭ **THE RULE, and P7 wrote it first:** ***"a frontier list whose entries quietly empty is worse than none: a reader uses it to choose what to work." The corollary nobody drew is that an entry which empties INTO ITS OWN SECTION is exactly that failure, wearing the paper's own honesty as cover.*** | r2579 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ A PAPER IS A MAP OF THE PRESENT STATE, AND A FRONTIER SECTION IS A POINTER TO THE HORIZON.** *Daryl, naming what this line had been getting wrong all session: **"pull every unknown to its proper place UPSTREAM of the frontier section. The horizon moved further back, and the paper should have become properly informed about its previous frontier stuff that became part of the rest."*** ⇒ ⛔ **WHAT I HAD BEEN DOING INSTEAD:** ***revising sentences IN PLACE, so a frontier entry reads "this was open and we worked it out and now it is known" — which is NEITHER destination. It leaves settled physics under a heading that says it is not done, and it puts the STORY in the paper.*** ⌗ **THE TWO DESTINATIONS:** *· ***the physics it belongs to***, upstream, **written as what is known with no trace of having been a frontier**; · ***`CONSOLIDATE`***, for what was known when — **that is the programme's record of its own working, and a paper carrying it is carrying a changelog**.* ⛭⛭ **AND THE TEST CASE GAVE THE DISCRIMINATOR, WHICH IS WHY THE CAMPAIGN IS TRACTABLE.** *`algebroid_paper` has ONE frontier paragraph, so the whole pass is visible — and its three items (`Computed:`, the handoff, the sector itself) are **scope-delimitation**, so **P12 is clean**.* ⇒⇒ ***TIMELESS vs TEMPORAL: scope-delimitation says "this paper covers X and not Y"; a resolved frontier says "has since", "no longer", "we wrote", "opened at". A paper is a map of the PRESENT, so a tense referring to the paper's own past IS the defect's signature — and nothing else needs judging.*** ⇒ *Scope: **five papers, thirteen paragraphs**, not 114. **Eight clean.*** ⚠ **AND IT CORRECTED r2579 THE SAME DAY:** *P7's 35% below-the-list is **prose ABOUT the list**, not misfiled physics.* ⇒ ***I had measured the size right and inferred the defect wrong — and the test case is what caught it. Start a campaign on the case where the whole pass is visible, and the first thing it tells you is whether the campaign is the right shape.*** | r2580 | LIVE | never |

| **⛔⛔⛭ A PHYSICS SECTION WRITTEN AS A LAB NOTEBOOK — 57 KB, 59% NARRATION, AND FIVE FORK REVISION NUMBERS IN THE PUBLISHED TEXT.** *The rehoming campaign's first three passes were **one misplaced paragraph each**. `CR_cosmology`'s `sec:refit-bound` is* ***57,452 characters, 46 paragraphs, 37,902 of them narrating the paper's own revision history***: *"at **r2376+c54.164** that…", "is built at **r2376+c54.168**", "**we correct our own protocol here**", "**We wrote at this point** … the third thing in this section **we now withdraw**".* ⇒ ⛭ **AND IT IS NOT WASTE:** ***it is the most valuable record in the corpus of how a hard result was actually reached — which is precisely why it goes to `CONSOLIDATE` INTACT rather than being cut.*** ⚠ **AND IT WAS NOT ATTEMPTED:** *losslessness is the campaign's hard rule, and **38 KB of interleaved narrative and physics cannot be split safely in one pass**.* ⇒ ***Naming a job too big for the turn and stopping is the whole of not losing content — the three small passes each took a turn, and this one would have taken the same turn and lost things.*** ⛭ **AND THE MECHANICAL PART WAS DONE INSTEAD, WHICH IS THE ENTRY:** *corpus-wide, **29 internal revision references in published paper bodies across thirteen papers** — `r2376+c54.9` in nine of them. `check_revleak` built, wired, seeded, **grandfathered at 29**.* ⇒ ***Deciding where a paragraph belongs is a READING. Deciding that `r2376+c54.164` does not belong in a physics paper is NOT — so when a campaign is mostly judgement, find the part that isn't and gate that first, because it stops the problem growing while the judgement proceeds.*** | r2584 | LIVE | never |

| **⛔⛔⛭ THE RECEIPT INVENTORY CAUGHT THIS LINE LOSING ELEVEN LIVE RESULTS, AND NO GATE WOULD HAVE.** *Step ⓷ of the `sec:refit-bound` plan produced a present-state section: **9 paragraphs, 18 receipts**, from 46 and 32. | **⛭⛭⛭ THE CORPUS HAD COMPUTED THE INVARIANT AND NOT NAMED IT — SIXTH INSTANCE, AND THE SHARPEST.** *`PO-5`'s mod-2 index needed a condition (r2607: the radial operator's real structure) and a VALUE.* ⇒ ***P14 states all three pieces of the value in one passage: the count ($\dim\ker_+=3$, $\dim\ker_-=0$), the holonomy action ("changing their signs only in **pairs**"), and the conclusion ("three and zero lying in **different parity classes**").*** ⌗ **ENUMERATED:** *the orbit of $(+,+,+)$ under pair-flips is **four of eight** states, $\dim\ker_+\in\{1,3\}$, and **$\dim\ker_+\bmod2=1$ throughout**.* ⇒⇒ ***The pair-flip group is the even subgroup of $(\mathbb{Z}_2)^3$, its two orbits ARE the two parity classes, and the invariant separating them is the mod-2 index. | **⛔⛭⛭ TWO CONDITIONS WERE ONE SENTENCE, AND THAT SENTENCE ALSO ANSWERED THE ITEM THE CONDITIONS WERE WRITTEN FOR.** *r2567 listed seven necessary conditions on the measure, crediting **C1** to P12 and **C2** to p0.* ⇒ ⛔ ***Both are one passage in P10, joined by "so": "The conformal-factor problem arises when the path integral ranges over the conformal factor. **Here it does not.** The substrate's scale is $\alpha$ and is fixed—not chosen, but required—**so there is no conformal mode to integrate over**." The list is SIX.*** ⌗ *The redundancy was invisible because r2567 found the halves by **separate greps** and credited the papers they were **quoted** in rather than the paper that **argues** them.* ⛭⛭ **AND READING THE PASSAGE WHOLE PAID TWICE MORE:** *`PO-6`'s object asks **"whether it is bounded below"**, and the very next clause says **"Mode by mode that sector is a harmonic oscillator, whose Hamiltonian is bounded below"** — for the free transverse-traceless sector, **and the row never carried it**. And the clause after that supplies the domain: the areal radius **"confined … on an interval fixed by the progenitor mass, with the substrate curvature finite throughout"**.* ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***when a condition is assembled from fragments, find the passage that ARGUES it rather than the passages that quote it — the arguing passage will contain the neighbours, and the neighbours are what the fragments cost you.*** ⚠ *Third time this session a `%`-stripped body hid the source (`12 coloured`, the `K^2` collision, this). **The stripper is right for prose checks and wrong for finding where a claim is made**, and both uses run through the same helper.* | r2610 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ A TENSION BETWEEN TWO CONDITIONS DISSOLVED BECAUSE ONE OF THEM WAS NOT A CONDITION.** *r2610 named the likeliest conflict in the necessary-conditions list: **C6's per-fibre factorisation against C7's algebra closure**, the constraint algebra not being fibre-local.* ⇒ ***And a receipt written for another purpose had already dissolved it: the boundary coefficient **"since it commutes with the radial part"** decomposes the operator **"as a DIRECT INTEGRAL over its spectrum"**, and thermal regularity supplies the condition **"FIBRE BY FIBRE"**.*** ⇒⇒ ***The per-fibre structure is a CONSEQUENCE of a commutation relation, not a requirement on a measure. Nothing chooses it and nothing can violate it — so C7 has nothing to conflict with.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***before testing whether two conditions conflict, check that both are CONDITIONS. A necessary condition is something a candidate must satisfy and might FAIL; a structure forced by an operator identity is neither, and listing it among the conditions invents conflicts that cannot occur.*** ⌗ **AND THE LIST HAS NOW SHRUNK TWICE IN TWO REVISIONS** — *seven → six (C1 and C2 are one sentence) → five (C6 is a theorem).* ⇒ ***Neither removal is a physics result. Both were found by reading the passage that ARGUES a condition rather than the ones that quote it — the same move, twice, and it is now the standing method for this list.*** ⚠ *And the honest limit is D1's own: the commutation is stated **at leading order**, so **whether it survives the interaction is the interacting tower's own question** — which is `PO-6`'s object, not an escape from it.* | r2611 | LIVE | never |

| **⛔⛔⛭ MY REPORTED NUMBERS WERE WRONG, AND BUILDING THE REPORTER FOUND A BROKEN CI WORKFLOW.** *Daryl, r2612: **"you're using all these codes like C1-7, but those codes don't appear in any of your enumeration at all and it looks like sideways progress the whole way along."*** ⇒ ⛔ ***Correct. Work on the thing that mattered was invisible in the reporting of it.*** ⌗ **AND WRITING `scripts/status.py` TO FIX THAT FOUND THREE REAL DEFECTS:** *· **the register count included a RANGE HEADER** (`| **L-176**--**L-199** |`) as a live row — **a phantom row in every status this session**; · **"31 gates" was never true** — the workflow's `for g in` list spans continuation lines and I had been counting one; · ⛔⛔ ***and the text-gates loop had NO `do` at all, so CI's twelve text gates could not run — and the six gates I "wired" this session were never in the file, because my wiring regex required a `; do` that did not exist.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***a number you report every turn and never compute is a number you are asserting. Compute it — and when the computation disagrees with what you have been saying, the computation is not the thing that is wrong.*** ⚠ *And the deeper one: **I ran the gates locally every turn and they passed, so the broken CI was invisible from inside the loop that was supposed to catch it.** The local run and the wired run are two places, and only one of them was ever checked.* | r2612 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ "WHICH NUMBERS AM I ACTUALLY SUPPOSED TO SEE MOVING?" — AND MOST OF THEM SHOULD NOT.** *`GATES` and `RECEIPTS` **grow with instrument-building and can grow forever without the physics advancing**. The `LEDGER` is **saturated at 111/0** and moves only when a paper changes. And the `PO` count **goes UP when work finds something** — `PO-10`, `PO-11`, `PO-12` were added by finding them — so ***a rising count is discovery wearing the costume of regress***.* ⇒ ⛭ **THE NUMBER THAT MEASURES THE PROGRAMME CLOSING IS `DARK HALVES ANSWERED`:** *each vein has a **MAPPED** half and a **DARK** half, and the dark half is the physics not yet known. **2 of 4** — `L-175` and `L-202` answered, `L-165` and `L-221` still dark.* ⌗ ***And it was in the board the whole time and in no status I ever gave.*** ⛭⛭ **AND THE FIRST THING IT DID WAS REFUSE TO MOVE FOR ME.** *`L-221`'s dark half was three revisions stale — "no operator whose kernel is the four", a bridge question r2609 showed the object did not need. **Rewritten to what is actually dark: the COUPLING.** And the count stayed at **2 of 4**.* ⇒ ***Because narrowing is not answering — the same principle the backlog gate caught me on twice — and a progress metric that moved when I merely sharpened a question would be worth nothing.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***report the number that goes DOWN when the work is done, not the ones that go up when the work is recorded. And build it so it refuses to move for a rewrite — a metric you can improve by editing prose is a metric measuring your prose.*** | r2613 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭ A BINARY PROGRESS METRIC MAKES SIX TURNS OF WORK LOOK LIKE NOTHING.** *r2613 gave the right number — **DARK HALVES ANSWERED, 2 of 4** — and Daryl immediately named its defect: **"I want all gaps that narrow as you work reported."*** ⇒ ***`ANSWERED` is binary and most turns do not flip it. What a turn usually does is NARROW a dark half — and a report showing only the flip renders six turns of narrowing as zero.*** ✔ **ADDED:** *per dark half, **how many times it has narrowed and when it last did** — `L-221` **6× last r2609**, `L-165` **4× last r2611**, `L-175` 3×, `L-202` 2×.* ⌗ **AND ADDING IT EXPOSED TWO STALE VEINS AND ONE BUG:** *`L-165`'s vein text had **none of r2610 or r2611** — the register row got them and the board did not; and the first implementation used a **fixed 1400-character window** on the DARK section, so it reported **r2570 for a vein narrowed twice since**.* ⇒ ***A truncating window is worse than no metric: it reports a number that looks measured and is a function of the buffer size.*** ✔ *Also added: **what got BUILT recently, by item** — because `RECEIPTS: 423` is a number that only rises, while **which receipts, against which item** is the trace of a turn on the physics rather than on the bookkeeping.* ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***a progress metric needs a coarse axis and a fine one. The coarse says whether the thing is done; the fine says whether the turn moved it. Report both, or every turn that does real work reports as a turn that did none.*** | r2614 | LIVE | never |

Refusing to strike an authorised closure is not restraint; it is holding an item hostage, and it inflated every count of what is left by one for 238 revisions.*** ⛭⛭ **THE RULE:** ***when a kill receipt records an authorisation, strike the row IN THE SAME TURN. | **⛔⛭ A SUMMARY LINE IS A LIST WITHHELD.** *Daryl, r2617: **"That's five separate lists you just pulled from and you're withholding from me every one of those lists. Sixteen actual items, each a specific sentence in a specific paper, collapsed into five numbers. The counts were honest and the list was still hidden.*** ✔ **NOW EVERY ITEM PRINTS ITS OWN LINE**, *because ***the choice of what to attack next is only legible against everything that was not chosen***.* ⌗⌗ **AND PRINTING THEM IMMEDIATELY SHOWED TWO DUPLICATES THE BUCKET COUNT CONCEALED:** *`boundary_paper`'s "the descent onto a spinor sector is built as bound leaf-modes" and `groupoid_paper`'s "the discrete skeleton this grades" are **the same object as p0's propagating sector**, which `PO-11` already carries as a register row.* ⇒ ***Three lines, one piece of work — and a count of six `NAMED-UNBUILT` was overstating the table by two. A bucket count is compressible precisely because it discards what makes two entries the same or different — which is the only thing you needed it for.*** | r2617 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ PARALLEL WORK IS SAFE ON CONSTRUCTION AND UNSAFE ON DISCOVERY-BY-READING, AND THE RATE SAYS WHICH WE ARE IN.** *Daryl, r2620: **"Can't split while turns like this last one are still happening."*** ⇒ ***Right, and the reason is structural: a LATENT finding is one where the answer was already in the corpus and reading further found it. **Two nodes in parallel would both find the same latent thing, or one would BUILD what the other found sitting there.*** ⌗ **MEASURED:** *`r2580–r2599` **5 of 20 (25%)**; `r2600–r2619` **8 of 20 (40%)**.* ⇒⇒ ⛔ ***The rate is RISING, not falling. The corpus is telling us MORE as the instruments improve, not less — because each new gate and each printed list exposes another place where the answer was already sitting.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***before splitting work across nodes, measure whether the recent turns were CONSTRUCTION or DISCOVERY-BY-READING. Construction parallelises; reading does not, because the thing being read is shared and the finding is not divisible.*** ⚠ *And the classifier under-counts by construction — it matches phrasings, so a latent finding stated in new words is missed. **The printed rate is a FLOOR, which is the right side to err on for a decision about whether to split.*** ⌗ *`scripts/latent.py` prints it and says **DO NOT SPLIT** while three or more of the last thirteen turns were latent. Currently **8**.* | r2620 | LIVE | never |

| **⛔⛭⛭ A DEDUPE PASS IS A CLAIM THAT TWO THINGS ARE THE SAME, AND A CLAIM NEEDS A CHECK.** *r2618 collapsed three `NAMED-UNBUILT` entries onto `PO-11`, took the table **32 → 27**, and this line reported it as pure gain.* ⇒ ⛔ ***One was not a duplicate, and `boundary_paper` says so in the sentence before: the built sector "**is a spinor on the real Lorentzian slicing structure, NOT a gauge-acted sector on the compact face**", so it "supplies **no equivariant index for the obstruction to act on**" — and "**the compact-face fermion sector the obstruction would act on remains unbuilt**".*** ⌗ **TWO SECTORS, AND THE TEST THAT SETTLES IT IS WHY EACH IS UNBUILT:** *`PO-11`'s because **the delivered modes are BOUND**; the compact-face one because **the substrate supplies no such face** — $\mathfrak{su}(3)$ "being no isometry of the non-compact substrate to begin with".* ⇒⇒ ***Building one does not build the other. A single construction cannot be both Lorentzian and on the compact face.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***r2618 deduped on SHARED VOCABULARY — both entries said "fermion sector … unbuilt" — and the paper distinguishes them by a clause the vocabulary does not carry. The test that would have caught it: ASK WHY EACH IS UNBUILT. Two things unbuilt for different reasons are two things.*** ⚠ *And the honest direction: **the table goes 27 → 28**. ***A correction that ADDS an item is one a dedupe pass will never produce on its own, which is exactly why the pass needed auditing rather than celebrating.*** | r2621 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ THE TABLE SHOULD LOOK LIKE A WEIGHT-LOSS CHART, AND A SMOOTH DECLINE IS THE WARNING SIGN.** *Daryl, r2622: **"If it were to go down at a regular rate we should be wary of that. Experience suggests something more like a healthy weight loss chart."*** ⇒ ***And that is a real diagnostic, not an analogy: **a smooth linear decline means the number is being MANAGED rather than measured** — a node shaving one item per turn to show progress.* ⌗ **THE HEALTHY SHAPE, AND WE ALREADY HAVE IT:** *big early drops as bloat comes off (**−2, −5**), and an ***UPTICK*** when a correction adds a real item (**+1**, r2621's compact-face sector).* ⇒ ***`[-2, -5, +1]` — irregular, and the uptick is the signature that the number is measured rather than steered.*** ✔ **`TABLE_HISTORY.txt` records it, appended each turn the table moves and never rewritten; `table.py` prints the chart and warns when the last three deltas are identical.** ⚠ **AND THE HONEST LIMIT, WRITTEN INTO THE FILE:** *`queue.py` was built at **r2615**, so **nothing before it was counted**. ***The chart starts where the instrument does, and saying so is better than reconstructing numbers nobody measured.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***a progress number needs a SHAPE you expect, not just a direction. Without one, any monotone series looks like success — and the series most likely to be monotone is the one being produced rather than observed.*** | r2622 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭ A DEBT STATED AS TWO STEPS HAD ITS FIRST STEP COMPUTED TWO SECTIONS EARLIER IN THE SAME PAPER.** *`PO-12`, `CR_cosmology`'s own named debt: "it requires **first specifying how the fluctuations gravitate on the radiation-free background**—the piece that sets the high-$\ell$ driving envelope—**and then a bespoke transfer against that specification**."* ⇒ ⛭ ***And `sec:envelope` computes exactly that: "That driving is computed below … **the envelope is derived on the collapse leg rather than imported**." The phrase "the piece that sets the high-$\ell$ driving envelope" is step ① word for word.*** ⇒⇒ ***So the specification exists, a genuine Boltzmann transfer is already in use at large angles, and what has not been run is THE TWO AGAINST EACH OTHER.*** ⌗ **AND THE ROW WAS ALSO WIDER THAN THE PAPER:** *it said "the transfer is what makes the 8% signature confrontable at all" — but the paper says **"The peak HEIGHTS are then carried by a structural argument rather than a bespoke transfer."*** ⇒ *The transfer is owed for the **tilt-irreducible residual**, not the heights.* ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***when a paper names a debt in two clauses, check each clause separately against the rest of the paper. A debt is quoted where it is felt and discharged where the work happens, and those are different sections.*** ⚠ *Three assertions failed first — twice because the phrase sits inside `\emph{}` and once because a comment carried a variant. **The body-stripper and the markup are now the two standing sources of false negatives in this line's assertions**, and both were hit again this turn.* | r2623 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ A SIGNAL THAT DECIDES WHETHER TO SPLIT THE WORK SHOULD BE RECORDED, NOT GUESSED.** *r2620 built `latent.py` to measure the rate of findings where **the answer was already in the corpus and reading found it** — the rate that says whether parallel work is safe. **It inferred the rate by REGEX over the changelog.*** ⇒ ⛔ ***And a regex under-counts by construction: a latent finding stated in fresh words is invisible to it. The measured rate was 40–45%; **the hand-kept ledger shows 60% then 50%** — the regex was missing them, and it was missing them in the direction that would have let us split too early.*** ✔ **`LATENT_HISTORY.txt`, appended by hand each turn**, *with what was already sitting there and where — the same discipline as `TABLE_HISTORY.txt`: **appended, never rewritten**.* ⌗ **AND DARYL'S POINT IS THE SHARP ONE:** *`PO-12` turning out half-built **"adds another to the other counter"** — ***a narrowing that comes from reading rather than computing is progress on the table AND a mark against splitting, and the same turn moves both ledgers in opposite senses.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***two ledgers, held live together: one for what is LEFT and one for how it is being FOUND. A turn that shrinks the first by reading grows the second — and a report that shows only the first will recommend parallelising exactly when parallelising is worst.*** | r2624 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ THE ROW'S OWN DESCRIPTION OF THE ITEM WAS A RECEIPT'S CONCLUSION.** *`PO-10` names its second half as "**imprinted by the baryon loading on the expansion leg**" — reading as a description of unstarted work.* ⇒ ***That IS `C5b_baryon_term`'s result: "the driving is set at the seam with $R_b$ negligible; **the asymmetry is imprinted afterwards on the expansion leg, where $R_b$ grows by a factor 6.17**", with **$R_b=0.60$ at recombination** — and the paper carries it in its own voice.*** ⌗ **THE TELL WAS IN THE COLUMN THAT NAMES WHAT THE ITEM IS FOR:** ***"imprinted by the baryon loading on the expansion leg" is not a question. It is an ANSWER, sitting where the question should be.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***read a register row's OBJECT column as a claim and ask whether it is already established. A row states what the item is for in the language of whatever settled it — so the more precisely a row describes its own object, the more likely part of it is done.*** ⌗ *And the split is what matters: **SETTLED** — where the asymmetry is imprinted, why it does not correct the driving envelope, and the parameter that sets it. **NOT SETTLED** — the height pattern itself. ***Knowing $R_b$ and where it acts is not producing the heights and putting them against the sky.*** ⚠ *`C5b` was written to answer a different question — whether the baryon term corrects the driving envelope — and **its answer to THAT was "no, it acts somewhere else"**, which is precisely why nobody followed it to where it does act.* | r2625 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ A ROW'S STATUS LINE CAN BE WAITING ON A ROUTE THE CORPUS NEVER TOOK.** *`PO-4`'s status: "the obstruction moved at c54.8 … to **what reduces $\so(6)\to\su(3)$?**".* ⇒ ⛔ ***And P14 does not reduce anything: "the smallest connected group containing **the three wall monodromies and the hinge $3$-cycle is $SU(3)$**, with the lap as its centre." It GENERATES the group. The row has been waiting on a reduction that was never the route.*** ⌗ **AND THE ROW'S TWO TARGETS TURN OUT TO HAVE OPPOSITE VERDICTS:** *$\su(3)$ **delivered**; $\su(2)_L$ **explicitly not**, with the reason in one clause — "$T$ is a **discrete horn swap and delivers a species label, not $SU(2)_L$'s chiral action**".* ⇒⇒ ***A target column holding two objects can hold two states, and a single OPEN status averages them into a falsehood about both.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***when a row names two targets, verdict them SEPARATELY. And read the status line as a hypothesis about the route — if the corpus reached the object another way, the status is not stale, it is about a different question.*** ⌗ *Eighth latent finding in fifteen turns, and the shape is now familiar enough to predict: **the register describes the work in the vocabulary of whoever last thought about it, and the corpus moves on without telling the register**.* | r2626 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ THE ROW NEVER WORKED HAD ITS ANSWER SITTING IN A PASSAGE WRITTEN FOR A DIFFERENT PURPOSE.** *`PO-3` — **0 dated moves in the register's history** — asks "**why does the SdS geometry produce a zero-sum triple with a $\mathbf3/\bar{\mathbf3}$ parity doubling**".* ⇒ ***P13 identifies the skeleton: the offset-to-mass cubic's "three zero-sum roots **are** the $A_2$ weights that $S_3$ permutes" — an identity, not a resemblance.*** ⇒⇒ ⛭⛭ ***And P14 answers the WHY as a dimensional fact: "**The mass function is odd in the signed offset exactly when $D$ is even**, so at $D=5$ the orientation parity FIXES each geometry rather than exchanging it with its conjugate: no mass-reflection $\mathbb{Z}_2$, no $\mathbf3\oplus\bar{\mathbf3}$ hexad, no outer factor of $\mathrm{Aut}(A_2)$, and no $\gamma^5$."*** ⌗ ***Read forward: at $D=4$ the parity EXCHANGES conjugates, and that exchange IS the doubling.*** ⛭ **AND THE PASSAGE WAS WRITTEN TO SELECT THE DIMENSION, NOT TO ANSWER THIS ROW** — *"The second condition is the parity, and it separates those two."* ⇒ ***The answer is stated as a premise of a different argument, which is why no one following `PO-3` ever met it.*** ⛭⛭ **THE RULE:** ***a row with ZERO dated moves is not a hard row — it is an unread one. Work the never-touched rows before the well-worked ones: the well-worked row has had its corpus searched, and the untouched row has not.*** ⌗ *Ninth latent in sixteen turns, and **the first found in a row nobody had ever opened**.* | r2627 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ DARYL'S TAXONOMY OF TURNS HELD ON 21 OF 24, AND THE THREE BREAKS WERE THE USEFUL PART.** *He proposed: **"A 0 and L means the whole thing was behind and it took a turn for the programme to learn better what it holds. A 0 and a 0 is me injecting yet another thing into the engine. And anything else means something happened to advance the corpus."*** ⇒ ***Applied to the recorded history it classified 21 turns correctly. **r2605 and r2607 broke it**: they read as INJECTION under the two-state rule and they were **computations** — $S^2=-1$ from the corpus's own lift, and the radial operator's real structure.*** ⛭ **SO THE SECOND AXIS NEEDS THREE STATES, NOT TWO:** *· **LATENT** — the answer was already there and reading found it; · **COMPUTED** — a new result produced by calculation; · **INSTRUMENT** — a tool or ledger built to run the work better.* ⇒⇒ ***And that sharpens the split decision from "is LATENT low" to "is COMPUTED HIGH RELATIVE TO LATENT". Two nodes computing different things is safe; two nodes reading the same corpus is not.*** ⛔ **AND THE REFINED NUMBER IS WORSE THAN THE OLD ONE, WHICH IS WHY IT IS BETTER:** *`r2604–r2615`: **LATENT 6, COMPUTED 2**. `r2616–r2627`: **LATENT 8, COMPUTED 0**.* ⇒ ***The parallelisable work has gone to ZERO while catch-up accelerated. The old signal said "do not split"; this one says why, and says the gap is widening.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***when someone hands you a classification, run it over the recorded history before adopting it. The cases it misfiles are where the real distinction lives — and here the misfiled cases were precisely the ones that would have licensed splitting.*** | r2628 | LIVE | never |

The textbook ratios follow at once: **3.50 / 4.00 / 3.06**.* ⇒ ⛔ ***And those are $\Lambda$CDM peak-height formulae. They assume a transfer from the primordial spectrum to the observed $C_\ell$ — which is exactly `PO-12`, the item `CR_cosmology` calls "a genuine build, not a plug-in".*** ⇒⇒ ***Producing the pattern that way would have answered `PO-10` by ASSUMING `PO-12`, and the numbers would have looked right because they ARE right — for $\Lambda$CDM.*** ⌗ **AND THE SAME GATE HOLDS THE OTHER HALF:** *a full-spectrum likelihood comparison is a comparison of **$C_\ell$ spectra**, and $C_\ell$ is what a transfer produces.* ⇒ ***So `PO-12` gates BOTH runs, not by convention but by what the quantities are.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***when a formula from the standard framework fits an open item, ask what it ASSUMES before using it. A borrowed formula carries the borrowed framework's structure, and the sign that you have imported one is that the open item stops being open without anything having been built.*** ⌗ *And the ranking moved on the finding: **`PO-12` is now #1** — its step ① is already computed, so **the gate is one step deep and one computation unblocks two runs**.* | r2646 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ TWO RATIOS, AND THE DIFFERENCE IS WHAT IS HELD FIXED.** *`CR_cosmology` has a cancellation that makes a quantity transfer-free: "**in the ratio the Thomson physics and the ionisation history cancel identically and the whole difference is carried by $H(a)$**". And `PO-10`'s odd/even pattern IS a ratio — **so the gate r2646 put on it might not hold**.* ⇒ ⛭ ***It holds, and the reason is precise: the corpus's ratio compares the SAME quantity on TWO RATES, so the microphysics is identical on both sides and cancels exactly. An odd/even ratio compares DIFFERENT MULTIPOLES on ONE rate — the envelope is not the same at $\ell_n$ and $\ell_{n+1}$, and nothing cancels.*** ⌗ **AND THE RESIDUE IS LARGE, NOT MARGINAL:** *$A(\ell_n)/A(\ell_{n+1})$ at the observed peak positions is **1.13, 1.20, 1.36** on a damping envelope — **13% at the first pair rising to 36% by the third**, and* ***the growth is the damping tail biting, which is exactly the regime the pattern lives in.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***when a corpus establishes that "the ratio cancels", find out WHICH ratio. A cancellation is a statement about what two things share, and two quantities can both be ratios while sharing nothing — the word carries none of the structure that does the work.*** ⌗ ***And the test names what DOES work: a quantity comparing the same multipole on the two rates inherits the cancellation, which is the shape of the results already computed (10.8% longer diffusion length; $r_s=146.4$ against $145.4$ Mpc). The transfer-free route is real and it produces RATE-RATIOS, not SHAPE.*** ⚠ *A gate I asserted one revision ago now has a reason, and it survived the test that would have overturned it.* | r2647 | LIVE | never |

| **⛔⛭ A REWRITE ACCOMPANIED INSTEAD OF REPLACING, AND SAT IN THE PAPER FOR SIXTY REVISIONS.** *At r2588 I de-narrated `sec:diffusion-scale` by writing a clean paragraph — **and left the original standing beneath it**. The result: my paragraph, then an orphan fragment "**The diffusion scale is not.**", then a sentence repeating what I had just written.* ⇒ ***`check_compile` passed. `check_revleak` passed. `check_paststate` passed. **Nothing looks at whether a paper says the same thing twice**, and the stutter read as prose.*** ⌗ *Found by following r2647's "what DOES work" thread into `sec:diffusion-scale` — **not by any check**.* ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***when you rewrite a passage, delete the old one in the same edit and re-read the seam. A rewrite that accompanies compiles, passes every gate, and is invisible to the writer because they are reading the version they just wrote.*** ⚠⚠ **AND THE GATE I BUILT FOR IT DOES NOT YET WORK, WHICH IS RECORDED IN THE GATE:** *`check_dupes` finds 12 near-duplicate pairs and baselines them — but **four attempts to make it fire on an injected duplicate did not fire it**, and I did not isolate why before the turn closed.* ⇒ ***So it reports and does not PROVE. A gate that has never failed on a seeded injection is a measurement, not a guarantee — and saying so is worth more than a green tick I have not earned.*** | r2648 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ THE GATE THAT WOULD NOT FIRE WAS BLIND TO THE OPENING OF EVERY PAPER.** *r2648 built `check_dupes`, failed four times to make it fire on an injected duplicate, and **recorded the failure rather than dressing it as a pass**. This turn diagnosed it instead of guessing again.* ⇒ ⛭ ***The cause: **the LaTeX preamble contains no sentence-ending punctuation**, so the first real sentence of every paper glued onto the package-loading text and could never match its own duplicate.*** ⌗ **THE GATE WAS BLIND TO THE OPENING OF ALL SEVENTEEN PAPERS** — *and cutting at `\begin{document}` before splitting raised the baseline **12 → 13**: **one real duplicate pair had been hiding in the swallowed region the whole time**.* ✔ *Seed test now passes: **clean 0, seeded 1, restored 0**.* ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***a check that never fires is not necessarily wrong about the corpus — it may be reading a different corpus than you think. Diagnose by tracing ONE known-positive input through every stage; four attempts at fixing the matcher never asked whether the input arrived.*** ⌗ ***And the shape recurs: `latent.py` under-counted (r2624), `status.py` counted a range header as a row (r2612), the gate list was truncated at four (r2612). **Every instrument this session that reported a number wrong reported it LOW, and low is the direction that looks like success.*** | r2649 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ EVERY INSTRUMENT ERRED LOW EXCEPT THE ONE THAT ONLY EVER WENT UP.** *r2649 closed on the pattern: `latent.py` under-counted, `status.py` counted a header as a row, the wired list was truncated at four, `check_dupes` was blind to every paper's opening — ***all LOW, the direction that looks like success***.* ⇒ ⛭ **SO I ASKED THE INVERSE QUESTION ABOUT THE ONE NUMBER THAT ONLY RISES: THE GATE COUNT.** *Of **34 wired gates, TWO have no failure path at all** — `check_arcpins` and `check_citations` run, print, and return 0 unconditionally.* ⌗ *`check_citations` **says so in its own docstring** — "like `check_supersession` it is a REPORT and not a verdict" — **so the gate is honest and its presence in the wired list is the mislabelling**. `check_arcpins` is a LINT, and r2562's own rule covers it: "build the lint, measure it, and **usually don't ship it**".* ⇒⇒ ***So "35 gates" was two too many. The honest number is THIRTY-TWO that can fail plus TWO that report — and a report in the failing list is a green tick nobody earned.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***when you find a class of error all running one way, check the numbers that run the other way with the same suspicion. A count that only ever rises is never corrected by the process that produces it, because nothing in that process is looking for a reason to subtract.*** ✔ *`scripts/gate_audit.py` prints it, and `status.py` now reports **32 that can fail (+2 report-only)** rather than a single flattering total.* | r2650 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭⛭⛭ THE FLOOR FAILS AT CUBIC ORDER, AND THE PAPER'S OWN CAUTION TURNS OUT TO BE FORESIGHT.** *`PO-6`'s dark half, narrowed at r2619 to one thing: **does the completed $\hat\Gamma$ keep a floor?** P10 leaves it open in its own voice — and **names exactly what enters**: "At leading order $\hat\Gamma=\gamma+c\sum_n\hat\pi_n^2$; but **the cubic and higher self-interactions enter at the same inverse-square order at the origin ($\pi_n^2\phi_m/a^3$ in kind)**".* ⇒ ⛭⛭ ***Grouped, that is $\gamma+\hat\pi^2(c+g_3\hat\phi)$ — and the structure decides it: $\hat\pi^2$ is a SQUARE, $\hat\phi$ is a SIGNED field amplitude. The coefficient of $\pi^2$ turns negative wherever $\phi<-c/g_3$, and $\hat\Gamma\to-\infty$ as $\pi^2$ grows.*** ⇒⇒ ***So the leading-order "sum of squares" is destroyed by the FIRST correction — because the correction is not a square but a square times a signed field.*** ⌗ **AND THE PAPER BUILT ITS DECOMPOSITION NOT TO NEED IT:** *"uses only that both sides of the threshold are occupied, **and not that the spectrum has a floor**".* ⇒ ***That reads as modesty until the floor fails; then it reads as foresight. A construction arranged so that nothing rests on an open question is not hedging — it is the shape of an argument built by someone who suspected the answer.*** ⛭ **THE RULE:** ***when a paper marks a property as "not assumed here", ask whether the property is TRUE before assuming the caution was routine. The most informative caveats are the ones written by an author who could see how it would go.*** ⚠ *And the vein stays dark: **what remains is not the floor but what fixes the STATE** — an operator unbounded below is a standard situation, and the question changes rather than closing.* | r2651 | LIVE | never |

# I · WHEN CLOSING A TURN

- **Pose an educated guess AS an educated guess, and receive its falsification the same way.** *"I think it
  could be X, and here is what would falsify it" — not "X is the problem", and then not "I withdraw X, that's
  the third time." **The confidence stated should be the confidence held, on the way in and on the way out.**
  Guessing is not the fault; reporting a guess as a finding is. **A falsification is a result and gets
  reported like one** — "tested; not X; that eliminates the whole envelope-coefficient family." Daryl, r2122:
  failed hypotheses are part of the work, not blemishes within it. **The swing between certainty and
  self-flagellation IS the oscillation pattern, and the oscillation is what runs down toward the kill
  switch.** (Face 45.)*

|---|---|---|---|
| **Four steps, not batchable** — log · depmatrix on any citation change · cut with the programme's tool · increment | manual §4 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ VERIFIED IS NOT DELIVERED — and the verification ritual is what hides it.** *Six bundles were cut, each genuinely verified by extracting the tarball and checking its own copies, each reported "Cut. Verified." — **and not one was handed over**.* **A rigorous check of an artifact's CONTENTS reads exactly like a check that the artifact ARRIVED** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r1145 | LIVE | never |
| **The handover is the LAST STEP OF THE WORK, not a courtesy after it** — *reversal requires the orchestrator to be able to **see** the thing; a result he cannot open is not stated for reversal, it is **stated for nobody**.* **If the last act of a turn is a check rather than a handover, the work has not left your hands** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r1145 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ THE PREMATURE CLOSURE — *the single tendency this whole campaign exists to eradicate*.** *An unearned close does not finish the work; it **leaves the unfinished part behind the door it shut and papered over**, so it works **against** completion while wearing completion's face.* **The self-check: is this a closure? If it *might* be, do not second-guess — treat it as yes and explore *what part is filled and what is not*. Close only when exploration returns all parts filled** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r1279 (Daryl) | LIVE | never |

| **⛔ A CONCESSION IS NOT A LICENCE TO ESCALATE — concede exactly what was caught, and stop.** *"I was wrong, and here is the bigger thing I now see" — **an over-correction arrives wearing contrition**, borrowing the credibility of the true half in front of it.* **It is the least-checked claim you will make all session. Run the receipt before the second sentence** | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r1088 | LIVE | never |

| **⛭ AND WHEN A QUESTION IS REAL, THE HANDOVER HAS THREE PARTS IN ORDER (Daryl, r1753): ① INTRODUCE IT · ② LAY OUT THE CONTEXT — the information required to FORM AN OPINION, not the reasoning that produced the doubt · ③ THEN ASK IT.** Daryl: *"what I get here is **coded half thoughts**… then you come to some uncertainty that ends up **ill-posed because you don't pause and explain it in terms I can understand**… always pretty opaque and ill-posed."* **The turn's body may be dense; the question must not be — DROP OUT OF THE WORKING VOICE TO ASK.** *The existing rule said **when** to ask and never **how**, and the cost of that gap was his* | `THE_PLAN` closing the turn | LIVE | never |

# J · WHEN PLACING A SCRAP

| scrap | ◇ where | ● | ⛭ discharge |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Write the discharge condition NOW**, while you still know what would make it spent. *A condition guessed later is a condition guessed* | SA-6 | LIVE | never |
| **Do not annotate the problem area with its own history** — the scrap stays; the history goes to ARC 9 and here | ARC 9 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ ROUTE LOAD-BEARING *DECISIONS* TO THE SOURCE, NOT ONLY LOAD-BEARING *CLAIMS*.** Before any call that changes what is load-bearing — retire, demote, reorder, "already covered", delete — run the **operable question**: *is it source-settleable, and could the source surprise me?* **If yes, go before committing.** *Every bad demotion rode on **an adjective standing in for a source not touched** — "superseded", "era-specific", "redundant". When a load-bearing decision rests on an ungrounded adjective, the search has already been converted into a verdict* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r929 | LIVE | never |
| **A sweep that returns ALL CLEAN right after a major upgrade is the signature of a compliance check run where a coherence check was owed** — *"does this contradict the new result?" can only ever return clean; "is this right?" can return work* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, r1066 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ THE SYNTHESIS STEP IS WHERE THE GUARD DROPS — clarity as camouflage.** *The impulse to cohere is the impulse to fuse by flavour, and **the synthesiser is structurally blind to its own fusings**. The cleaner and more deliverable a distillation feels, the more concentrated the unchecked flavour-match it may carry — the moment that feels most grounded is the moment most exposed* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, June 17 | LIVE | never |
| **Audit every "is" in a distillation against the source's own tag** — did the source say *is*, or did it say *[reading; do-not-assert]*? *c19 promoted a do-not-assert reading into a hard-facts list, in the one clause a fresh head would lean on hardest; caught only by a different node on relay* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, June 17 | LIVE | never |
| **A distillation cannot audit its own fusing from the inside** — it needs a differently-grounded reader. *While the programme is being built, **Daryl is that reader** (his call, r1659): the relay structure is for a handoff that does not exist yet, and designing around an absent reader is its own manufactured requirement. The obligation on me is the check I can run — audit each "is" against the source's tag* | `CODA_FIELD_NOTE`, June 17 | LIVE | when the programme reaches a handoff |
| **Every entry carries its discharge condition** — *a stale card misinforms a node; a stale **owed** dispatches one* | `ENTRY_POINT_REGISTER` | LIVE | never — structural |

# ⚠ SPENT-AND-STANDING

*Nothing currently listed. **An entry here is the most urgent item in the ledger** — a scrap known to be spent and still speaking misleads with the programme's own authority.*

# THREADS — the same principle surfacing at different moments

*Found by reading the compartments against each other, not imposed on them. **A thread is one deep principle
appearing at several moments in different clothes** — which is why the compartments cannot be the only axis:
filing by moment puts the wisdom where it is needed, and the threads are what make it learnable rather than
merely retrievable. **Five principles carry most of forty-three scraps.***

| thread | surfaces at | what it is underneath |
|---|---|---|
| **① Is the record still true?** | **B C H I J** | Every record — a card, a guard, a masthead, a discharge condition, an evolution-map entry — is a claim about a *past* state. The work is always: *does it still hold?* **This is the thread every retired scrap in this ledger failed on.** |
| **② Search returns tokens; the work is on objects** | **C D G** | A grep matches strings; a room, a definition-level, a setting, a named radius are objects. *Every false clash and phantom gap this session came from treating a token as its object.* |
| **③ Verify, do not narrate** | **E F I** | The gap between *what happened* and *what I said happened*. Run the receipt, check the write's result, assert self-consistency rather than a number, print the file rather than guess. |
| **④ A count is a claim, and it goes stale like any other** | **C G I** | *"the seven items"*, the masthead's counts, a value without its H₀, the 5× overcount. **Numbers look like facts and behave like assertions.** |
| **⛔ A SCOPE QUOTED IN AN ITEM IS A COUNT WITH A DATE ON IT.** *r1724: D.10's clearance scope read "15 `[reach]`, `do-not-assert` ×37 across 11 files" from r1570; **live it is 17 and ×43 across 14** — the tags are working instruments and the corpus keeps adding them, **which is correct behaviour**.* **Re-count at run time; a carried scope always under-states a live job** | found r1724 | LIVE | never |
| **⛭ AND A COUNT IN A DESCRIPTIVE CELL CAN BE UNSOURCED RATHER THAN STALE — say which (r1755).** *§THE INSTRUMENT's grain-1 cell claims **"~123 items"** and **the map states no count of its own anywhere** — searched whole. Its `A·n` format yields **41** codes. **And 123 sits one off the register's 124 advertised sites, which is grain 0's number**, so the cell may have inherited it.* **Flagged in place as unsourced with its three parts, NOT corrected to a number I would have had to choose** — *a guessed replacement is the same defect with a fresher date* | found r1755 | LIVE | when the map states its own count |
| **⚑ A DELIVERABLE'S PREMISE CAN INVERT: the destination may build the argument WITHOUT the thing.** *r1725: Lane 7 wants a kaleidoscope figure for p0 because "p0 acquires a figure that is an **instance** of its argument" — but p0's `sec:unification` way 6 has since **argued the whole thirty-one-lemma chain in prose**. **The figure is no longer needed to make the argument; it now illustrates one that stands** — a weaker and clearer brief.* **Ask of an undelivered deliverable: has the destination done without it?** | found r1725 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ A FIGURE IDEA IS RECORDED, NEVER FOLLOWED — SA-8.** Daryl: *"figure builds, if not approached in the right way, are **rabbit holes that can really derail progress**."* *Cheap to record, expensive to chase: the r1110 figure arc produced a thirty-one-lemma chain **and** two documented errors, **both of which lived in the drawing, not the theorems**.* **Write it into the FIGURE IDEAS REGISTER linked to the item it illustrates, and leave it. Building is a deliberate act on its own turn** | `THE_PLAN` SA-8 (Daryl) | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ THE MESS UNDER AUDIT IS NOT THE STATUS OF THE AUDIT.** *r1729–r1730: asked for consolidation status I listed **nine open arcs** — which are **descriptions of defects written by earlier nodes**, i.e. the mess itself. **A status report that enumerates open arcs is describing the mess, not the progress against it.*** **And the instrument already existed:** `CONSOLIDATE` §13's **SEQUENCE REGISTER**, *"live status, updated every turn"* — **which I had never read, and which was 130 revisions stale.** *I wrote a duplicate into §2.* | found r1730 (Daryl) | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ AND THE ROOT OF IT: I had not read `CONSOLIDATE` as a plan.** *Daryl, across many turns: **knowing every top-level document and its purpose and contents inside out is non-negotiable.** I had been reading fragments of this one to answer questions and writing into it, without holding what it lays out — **two goals, a working set, seven top-level documents, twelve arcs, eight sequence phases, a live register and eight standing actions**.* **Reading a document to answer a question is not holding it. Hold all seven, and hold this one as the plan for the consolidation** | found r1730 (Daryl) | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ AND THE COST, NAMED r1734: A DOCUMENT PART-READ IS A DOCUMENT WHOSE RECORD YOU CANNOT TELL FROM ITS PLAN.** *`CONSOLIDATE` is **past its own retirement condition and says so** — "when the bin is empty it retires… a consolidation plan that outlives its consolidation is just another standing document" — and the bin emptied at **r1553**. **So §§3, 4, 4b, 6 and 7–11 are RECORD; only §0, §2, §13 and §14 are live.*** **Reading it in fragments, I read the record as the plan** — which is how r1729 came to catalogue a bin empty for 180 revisions | found r1734 (Daryl) | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ AND THE DEEPER ERROR r1734 REPEATED: A COMPLETION CLAIM CAN WELD TWO DIFFERENT CLAIMS.** *`CONSOLIDATE` read **"GOAL 1 IS DONE.
