| **⛭ AND COUNT THE HEADING AFTER WRITING IT — the opposite failure, found r1778.** *§12's warning covers entries **LOST** to a bad anchor (27 across two breaks). **r1777 carried TWO entries under one number**, both mine, both written that turn, independently composed, **neither wrong and neither a superset**.* **A duplicate is quieter than a loss — the log looks fuller, not emptier.** *Fix: `grep -c` the revision heading, expect one; and **keep both bodies under one heading rather than deleting an account*** | found r1778 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ THE PERIMETER DEFECT — the class every local discipline is blind to (`THE_METHOD` §4b).** *A boundary drawn at the **wrong radius**: every local statement correct, the paper **locally right everywhere**, so **a joint-by-joint pass returns clean and the clean is honest — that is the trap**.* **Diagnostic: if every local check confirms and the dissonance persists, the defect is not in a joint — ask where the boundary sits relative to the whole.** *Tells: an entry reversing repeatedly while each reversal verifies cleanly; a sweep returning all-clean right after a major upgrade.* **And it binds dispatch: never farm a coherence judgement — only a coherence check can return work; a sweep that cannot return work is not a sweep** | `THE_METHOD` §4b | LIVE | never |
| **⛭ AND THE PROGRAMME'S DEEPEST AIM, which only `THE_METHOD` states: acutely aware decision-making that can run autonomously.** *One move: **build the generically operable questions that resolve a decision at the source, ask them as you go, and track every step stated-for-reversal so the trail can be picked up anywhere.*** *The operable questions are the forward face, tracking-and-reversibility the recovery face — together **what lets a thing with no persistent memory be trusted to decide on its own**.* **The live project: externalizing the operable questions out of Daryl's intuition into explicit runnable checks — "each intuition he has to supply becomes a question the instance asks itself, pre-commit, every time"** | `THE_METHOD` frame | LIVE | never |
| **⚑ AND THE TEST THAT KEEPS THE REGISTER HONEST: a figure ENRICHES what is argued; it does not carry the argument.** *So **an entry that looks *needed* for an argument is a signal the argument is not yet written** — which makes it a physics item, not a figure one* | `THE_PLAN` SA-8, from r1725 | LIVE | never |
| **⛔ AND CHECK THAT A PASS REACHED THE LEDGER IT FEEDS.** *`JARGON_LEDGER` has **three entries, newest r1310**, while the idiom pass that fills it **ran on all seventeen papers by r1406**. **A ledger with one row for a term that has 43 sites is an index entry, not a gathering.*** **"The pass ran" and "the pass's findings landed" are two claims** | found r1724 | LIVE | never |
| **⑤ Placement is local and right; the hub is what was missing** | **H J** | The scrap belongs where it protects. What never existed was the thing that knows where they all are — which is this ledger, and the reason it exists at all. |

**Threads are a hypothesis about structure, held loosely.** If one turns out to be the better *primary* filing —
if a node reaches wisdom faster by principle than by moment — **re-file on it.** *An intermediary categorisation
that has done its work and been outgrown should go, and the record of why it was right at the time stays.*

---

# THE LEDGER'S OWN LIFECYCLE — this document is under revision too

*The scrap lifecycle is below. **This is the ledger's**, and it exists because a wisdom ledger that cannot be
restructured becomes exactly what it was built to prevent: a series of snapshots, consulted only once someone
notices it has gone stale itself.*

**Signals that the structure — not the contents — wants work.** Any one of these is a finding, not an annoyance:
- **A scrap fits no compartment.** *Then a moment of the work is unnamed.* Do not force it; name the missing
  compartment. *(This is `THE_METHOD` §7's third branch applied to the ledger: no home → name that.)*
- **A compartment grows past reading.** *Then it is really two moments, and the split will tell you which.*
- **A scrap keeps being looked for in the wrong compartment.** *The filing is wrong, not the reader.*
- **A thread's compartments are always consulted together.** *Then the thread may be the better axis.*
- **A compartment empties.** *Either that moment stopped mattering, or its wisdom went uncaptured — and those
  are opposite findings, so check which.*
- **The operating layer changes shape.** The compartments are the operating layer's own moments; **if it gains
  or loses one, this gains or loses one.** *The two are meant to move together — that is the whole design.*

**How a restructure happens.** Same discipline as any other change here: state what is wrong with the current
structure, propose the new one, **check the new one against the existing entries before adopting it** (every
entry must still have a home, and no entry two), then re-file and **record the reasoning** — not just the
result. *A structure changed without its reasoning kept is a structure that will be changed back.*

**Restructurings so far**
| when | from → to | why |
|---|---|---|
| **r1641** | — → flat lifecycle list (RETIRED / SPENT-AND-STANDING / LIVE) | first build; the object had just been named |
| **r1644** | flat list → **ten compartments by moment of use** | *"you can only reach it by grepping, which means you only find what you already suspect."* The list preserved the wisdom and did not deliver it. Compartments taken from the operating layer's own moments so the two move together |
| **r1645** | + **threads** and **this section** | the compartments made the wisdom reachable; the threads make it learnable, and without a lifecycle of its own the ledger would go stale exactly as its contents do |

---

# The patterns — the axis the eventual sweep runs on

*Each retired scrap above instantiates one. These are what `THE_PLAN`'s proposed canon-sweep sweeps for.*

1. **The guard that outlives its defect.** Worst form: it dispatches a node to break what is now correct. *(bibkey r1566→r1624; the P14 guard r968→r1638.)*
2. **The per-paper one-off** — defined locally, never promoted, while other papers invent their own. *(`r_N` in P12 alone.)*
3. **The collapsed definition level** — a geometry-specific value read as a definition. *(seam.)*
4. **The ledger that starts defining** — a reporting document drifting into canon.
5. **The reference that names its own reading order incompletely.** *(`README` step 8.)*

**And the observation the retired list forces: not one of them was found by looking for it.** Every one turned up by accident, while doing something else. *That is the argument for this ledger, made by its own contents — and it is why the filing is by moment-of-use: so the next one is met by a node doing the work, not by a node who happened to grep the right word.*

---

# How a scrap moves

1. **Placed** at the site that needs it, as always. *This does not change.*
2. **Entered here** by SA-6 as a by-product, **in the compartment for the moment it applies**, with its discharge condition written at the moment of placing.
3. **Updated** when the programme moves under it but it still has work to do.
4. **Retired** when its condition is met — struck at its site, moved to RETIRED **with its lesson**, and the three-stage check run so every document carrying it knows.

**The lesson is the reason for the retirement, not a consolation for it.** *A programme that keeps only its current state has thrown away the reason it is in that state.*

## ⌗ r2376+c54.13 — **A NARROW RESULT MAY NARROW A CLAIM'S SCOPE. IT MAY NOT CLOSE A SEARCH.**

**What it protects against.** *The move that fires when a probe returns something clean and adjacent to an open
question: chain it with two other sound, narrowly-scoped results and read the conjunction as a verdict on the
question. **Each piece is true. The conjunction is not an argument.***

**The instance.** *A sweep computed that the horizon roots' **sign** $2{+}1$ is species-graded and tracks the
parity of $D$; added that $\mathrm{sign}(r)$ is species by §0; added the taxonomy's level-(3) wall — and
concluded that `FIGURE` **R3** ($2{+}1\to uud$) was dead, and the Nariai/three-quark lead with it.* ***P14 names
TWO $2{+}1$s in a single sentence*** *— the **designation** one ($\sigma$'s, which is a flavour-side structure)
and the **sign** one — **and R3 names neither**. The computation was about the other object.*

**Why it is worth a scrap.** *This is **face 24**, the cyanide face, and it fired **at the same drill-site the
taxonomy had already caught it at r696** — a site that document explicitly says to* **"drill hardest"** *and
holds* **open**. *A failure mode that recurs at one location is a property of the location: **an open search
sitting adjacent to a clean computation is where this face lives.***

**The rule.** ***Before a result is allowed to bear on an open question, check that it is about the same
object.*** *And when a question does not specify its object — as R3 did not — **the first work owed is to make
it specify, not to answer it**.*

**Discharge condition.** *None; standing. **The tell is a verdict on an open search arriving as a by-product of
a probe run for another purpose.***
